```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nephelai adalah nimfa awan dan hujan dalam mitologi Yunani yang membawa air Okeanos ke langit dalam kendi dari awan.",
    "en": "The Nephelai are the Greek nymphs of clouds and rain who carry the water of Oceanus to the sky in jugs of cloud.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nefeles-c01",
      "nefeles-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Nephelai, dari kata Yunani néphos, \"awan\", adalah nimfa awan dan hujan dalam mitologi Yunani. Mereka biasanya dianggap putri Okeanos dan Tethys, meski Aristophanes menyebut mereka putri Aither. Mereka naik dari sungai Okeanos yang mengelilingi dunia, membawa airnya ke langit dalam kendi dari awan, lalu menghidupi Bumi dan mengisi sungai-sungai saudara mereka.",
      "en": "The Nephelai, from Greek néphos, 'cloud', are the nymphs of clouds and rain in Greek mythology. They are usually daughters of Oceanus and Tethys, though Aristophanes makes them daughters of Aether. They rise from the world-encircling river Oceanus, carry its water to the sky in jugs of cloud, then nourish the Earth and feed their brother rivers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nefeles-c01",
        "nefeles-c02",
        "nefeles-c04",
        "nefeles-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Seperti para naiad, mereka digambarkan sebagai gadis cantik yang menuangkan air dari kendi. Mereka mungkin juga termasuk pengiring Artemis.",
      "en": "Like the naiads, they are pictured as beautiful girls pouring water from jugs. They may also be among Artemis's attendants.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nefeles-c03",
        "nefeles-c05"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c01",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la mythologie grecque, les Néphélées (en grec ancien Νεφέλαι / Nephélai, de νέφος / néphos, « nuage »), filles d'Éther ou d'Océan et de Téthys, sont les nymphes des nuages et de la pluie.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Yunani, Nephelai (dari kata Yunani néphos, \"awan\"), putri Aither atau Okeanos dan Tethys, adalah nimfa awan dan hujan.",
        "en": "In Greek mythology the Nephelai (from Greek néphos, 'cloud'), daughters of Aether or of Oceanus and Tethys, are the nymphs of clouds and rain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c02",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s1",
      "quote": "Elles s'élèvent des flots de leur père, le grand fleuve Océan qui entoure le monde, emportant avec elles vers le Ciel, ses eaux fraîches dans des cruches faites de nuages. De ces eaux, elles nourrissent la Terre et alimentent les eaux de leurs frères, les dieux fleuves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka naik dari arus ayah mereka, sungai besar Okeanos yang mengelilingi dunia, membawa airnya ke langit dalam kendi dari awan, lalu dengan air itu menghidupi Bumi dan mengisi sungai-sungai saudara mereka.",
        "en": "They rise from the waters of their father, the great river Oceanus that encircles the world, carrying its cool water to the sky in jugs of cloud, and with it nourish the Earth and feed their brothers the river gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c03",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s1",
      "quote": "Les Néphélées, tout comme leurs sœurs les naïades, sont dépeintes comme de belles jeunes filles tenant des cruches, qui versent de l'eau.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti saudari mereka para naiad, Nephelai digambarkan sebagai gadis cantik yang memegang kendi dan menuangkan air.",
        "en": "Like their sisters the naiads, the Nephelai are pictured as beautiful girls holding jugs and pouring water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c04",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s2",
      "quote": "Sin embargo Aristófanes, en su obra Las nubes, afirmaba que eran hijas del Éter, personificación del cielo superior.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aristophanes dalam komedi Awan menyebut mereka putri Aither, personifikasi langit atas.",
        "en": "Aristophanes, in his play The Clouds, said they were daughters of Aether, personification of the upper sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c05",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s2",
      "quote": "Estas ninfas también pudieran ser las asistentas de Artemisa, aunque no se especifica que sean ninfas de las nubes",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nimfa-nimfa ini mungkin juga para pengiring Artemis, meski tidak disebutkan bahwa mereka nimfa awan.",
        "en": "These nymphs may also be Artemis's attendants, though they are not specified as cloud nymphs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nefeles-c06",
      "source_id": "nefeles-s3",
      "quote": "Na mitologia grega, as néfeles (em grego Νεφέλαι (Nefélai), \"nuvens\") eram as ninfas das nuvens e da chuva. Eram ninfas oceânides, isto é, nascidas de Oceano e de Tétis.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Portugis, para nefele adalah nimfa awan dan hujan dari golongan Okeanid, putri Okeanos dan Tethys.",
        "en": "According to the Portuguese article, the nephelai were nymphs of clouds and rain, Oceanids born of Oceanus and Tethys."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ojáncanu adalah raksasa bermata satu dalam mitologi Cantabria, perwujudan kekejaman, yang hanya dapat dibunuh dengan mencabut rambut putih di janggutnya.",
    "en": "The Ojáncanu is a one-eyed giant of Cantabrian mythology, an embodiment of cruelty, killed only by pulling the white hair from its beard.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ojancanu-c01",
      "ojancanu-c02",
      "ojancanu-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ojáncanu adalah raksasa mirip kiklops dalam mitologi Cantabria dan perwujudan kekejaman. Ia bermata satu, setinggi 10 sampai 20 kaki, sangat kuat, berjari sepuluh di tiap tangan dan kaki, dan bergigi dua baris. Ia tinggal di gua-gua suram di daerah terpencil La Montaña. Pasangannya disebut ojáncana.",
      "en": "The Ojáncanu is a cyclops-like giant of Cantabrian mythology and the embodiment of cruelty. It is one-eyed, 10 to 20 ft tall, immensely strong, with ten digits on each hand and foot and two rows of teeth. It lives in gloomy caves in remote parts of La Montaña. Its female is called the ojáncana.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ojancanu-c01",
        "ojancanu-c02",
        "ojancanu-c06",
        "ojancanu-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia terus berbuat jahat: mencabut batu, merusak gubuk, dan menyumbat mata air, dan selalu menang melawan beruang dan banteng. Ia hanya takut pada Anjana, juga pada katak terbang dan burung hantu. Cara termudah membunuhnya adalah mencabut satu-satunya rambut putih di janggutnya. Ojáncanu tidak kawin; jasad yang tua dikubur di bawah pohon ek atau yew.",
      "en": "It constantly does evil, pulling up rocks, wrecking huts and blocking springs, and always beats bears and bulls. It fears only the Anjanas, as well as flying toads and barn owls. The easiest way to kill it is to pull the single white hair from its beard. Ojáncanus do not mate; an old one's corpse is buried under an oak or yew.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ojancanu-c03",
        "ojancanu-c04",
        "ojancanu-c05",
        "ojancanu-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c01",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s1",
      "quote": "The Ojáncanu (Cantabrian: [oˈhankanu]) is a cyclops-like giant found in Cantabrian mythology, and is an embodiment of cruelty and brutality.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ojáncanu adalah raksasa mirip kiklops dalam mitologi Cantabria, perwujudan kekejaman dan kebrutalan.",
        "en": "The Ojáncanu is a cyclops-like giant of Cantabrian mythology, an embodiment of cruelty and brutality."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c02",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s1",
      "quote": "It appears as a 10 to 20 ft. one-eyed giant with superhuman strength, with hands and feet that contain ten digits each, and two rows of teeth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia raksasa bermata satu setinggi 10 sampai 20 kaki dengan kekuatan luar biasa, berjari sepuluh di tiap tangan dan kaki, dan bergigi dua baris.",
        "en": "It appears as a one-eyed giant 10 to 20 ft tall with superhuman strength, ten digits on each hand and foot, and two rows of teeth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c03",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s1",
      "quote": "Apparently the easiest way of killing an Ojancanu is to pull the single white hair found in its mess of a beard.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cara termudah membunuh Ojáncanu konon dengan mencabut satu-satunya rambut putih di janggutnya yang kusut.",
        "en": "The easiest way to kill an Ojáncanu is said to be pulling out the single white hair in its tangled beard."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c04",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s1",
      "quote": "He is constantly doing evil deeds such as pulling up rocks, destroying huts and trees, and blocking water sources. He fights Cantabrian brown bears and Tudanca bulls, and always wins. He only fears the Anjanas, the good Cantabrian fairies.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia terus berbuat jahat, seperti mencabut batu, merusak gubuk dan pohon, dan menyumbat mata air; ia selalu menang melawan beruang cokelat dan banteng Tudanca, dan hanya takut pada Anjana, peri baik Cantabria.",
        "en": "He constantly does evil, pulling up rocks, wrecking huts and trees and blocking springs; he fights brown bears and Tudanca bulls and always wins, fearing only the Anjanas, the good fairies of Cantabria."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c05",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s1",
      "quote": "Instead of mating when an old Ojancanu dies, the others distribute the insides and bury the corpse under an oak or yew tree.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka tidak berkembang biak dengan kawin; ketika Ojáncanu tua mati, yang lain membagi isi perutnya dan mengubur jasadnya di bawah pohon ek atau yew.",
        "en": "Instead of mating, when an old Ojáncanu dies the others share out its insides and bury the corpse under an oak or yew."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c06",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s2",
      "quote": "De carácter salvaje, fiero y vengativo, esta criatura habita en las profundas y lúgubres grutas de los parajes más recónditos de La Montaña, cuyas entradas suelen estar cerradas con maleza y grandes rocas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk liar, buas, dan pendendam ini tinggal di gua-gua dalam dan suram di tempat paling terpencil di La Montaña, yang pintunya tertutup semak dan batu besar.",
        "en": "This wild, fierce and vengeful creature lives in deep, gloomy caves in the remotest parts of La Montaña, their entrances blocked with brush and boulders."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c07",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s2",
      "quote": "Por otro lado la tradición dice que tienen mucho miedo a los sapos voladores y a las lechuzas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tradisi juga menyebut mereka sangat takut pada katak terbang dan burung hantu.",
        "en": "Tradition also says they greatly fear flying toads and barn owls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ojancanu-c08",
      "source_id": "ojancanu-s3",
      "quote": "Ojáncanu estas virseksulo kaj ĝia edzino estas in-seksa ojáncana. Ilia kontraŭulo estas anjana, bonfarema feino en la kantabra mitaro, kiun ili timas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel Esperanto, Ojáncanu jantan dan pasangannya disebut ojáncana; lawan mereka adalah anjana, peri baik Cantabria yang mereka takuti.",
        "en": "According to the Esperanto article, the Ojáncanu is male and its female is the ojáncana; their opponent is the anjana, the benevolent Cantabrian fairy they fear."
      }
    }
```

```json
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "olanoiko-zakurra-c01",
      "source_id": "olanoiko-zakurra-s2",
      "quote": "Olanoiko zakurra ('el gos d'Olanoi', del basc Olanoiko 'd'Olanoi' i zakurra 'el gos') és un geni de la mitologia basca que adopta l'aparença d'un gran gos gris i que és el guardià de la cova Olanoi de Beizama.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Olanoiko zakurra (\"anjing Olanoi\") adalah roh mitologi Basque berwujud anjing abu-abu besar yang menjaga gua Olanoi di Beizama.",
        "en": "Olanoiko zakurra ('the dog of Olanoi') is a spirit of Basque mythology in the form of a big grey dog, guardian of the Olanoi cave at Beizama."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olanoiko-zakurra-c02",
      "source_id": "olanoiko-zakurra-s2",
      "quote": "És un més dels nombrosos genis de la mitologia basca zoomorfs (Aatxe, Zezengorri, Akerbeltz, etc.) que viuen en coves i que, sovint, ataquen a qualsevol que s'hi acosti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia salah satu dari banyak roh berwujud hewan dalam mitologi Basque, seperti Aatxe, Zezengorri, dan Akerbeltz, yang tinggal di gua dan sering menyerang siapa pun yang mendekat.",
        "en": "It is one of the many animal-shaped spirits of Basque mythology, like Aatxe, Zezengorri and Akerbeltz, that live in caves and often attack anyone who approaches."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olanoiko-zakurra-c03",
      "source_id": "olanoiko-zakurra-s3",
      "quote": "Beizamako Olanoi kobazuloko zaindaria da txakur irelu handi hau, eta bertara hurbiltzen diren lagunei eraso egiten die.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh anjing besar ini penjaga gua Olanoi di Beizama dan menyerang orang yang mendekat.",
        "en": "This great dog spirit guards the Olanoi cave at Beizama and attacks those who come near."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olanoiko-zakurra-c04",
      "source_id": "olanoiko-zakurra-s1",
      "quote": "Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra et Mirokutana sont des génies de la mythologie basque qui ont l'aspect d'un chien.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra, dan Mirokutana adalah roh mitologi Basque yang berwujud anjing.",
        "en": "Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra and Mirokutana are spirits of Basque mythology that look like dogs."
      }
    }
```

```json
  "disposition": {

      "pard-c05"

  },
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Pard adalah kucing besar berbintik dalam bestiari abad pertengahan yang dianggap kawin dengan singa dan, sejak abad ke-13, makhluk haus darah yang hampir iblis.",
    "en": "The pard is a spotted big cat of medieval bestiaries believed to mate with lions and, from the 13th century, seen as a bloodthirsty, almost demonic creature.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pard-c01",
      "pard-c05",
      "pard-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pard, dari kata Yunani Kuno untuk macan tutul, tercantum dalam Naturalis Historia karya Plinius Tua dan bestiari abad pertengahan. Plinius menulis bahwa cheetah lahir dari singa betina yang kawin dengan pard, dan bahwa singa jantan membalas dendam bila mencium bau pard pada pasangannya. Isidorus dari Sevilla menggambarkan bulunya berbintik seperti jerapah dan menyebutnya haus darah.",
      "en": "The pard, from the Ancient Greek word for the leopard, appears in Pliny the Elder's Natural History and in medieval bestiaries. Pliny writes that cheetahs were born of lionesses mating with pards, and that the male lion takes revenge when he smells a pard on his mate. Isidore of Seville describes its coat as mottled like a giraffe's and calls it bloodthirsty.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pard-c01",
        "pard-c02",
        "pard-c03",
        "pard-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada abad ke-13, terutama lewat Bestiari MS Bodley 764, pard menjadi makhluk haus darah yang hampir iblis, dengan bintik yang melambangkan dosa. Dalam bestiari ia juga disebut pardus, kucing besar yang sangat cepat yang kawin dengan singa untuk menghasilkan leopard.",
      "en": "In the 13th century, chiefly through the MS Bodley 764 Bestiary, the pard became a bloodthirsty, almost demonic creature whose spots symbolised sin. In bestiaries it is also called the pardus, an extremely fast big cat that mates with lions to produce leopards.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pard-c05",
        "pard-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

      "author": null,

      "published": null,

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "pard-c01",
      "source_id": "pard-s1",
      "quote": "Pard (Ancient Greek: πάρδος) is the Ancient Greek word for the leopard, which is listed in medieval bestiaries and in Pliny the Elder's book Natural History.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pard (Yunani Kuno: πάρδος) adalah kata Yunani Kuno untuk macan tutul, yang tercantum dalam bestiari abad pertengahan dan Naturalis Historia karya Plinius Tua.",
        "en": "Pard (Ancient Greek πάρδος) is the Ancient Greek word for the leopard, listed in medieval bestiaries and in Pliny the Elder's Natural History."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pard-c02",
      "source_id": "pard-s1",
      "quote": "Pliny writes about the origin of cheetahs (though he does not have a clearly separate word for these animals) which were believed to be a hybrid animal (like mules or ligers) resulting from the union between a promiscuous lioness and the pard, i.e. the leopard.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Plinius menulis bahwa cheetah dipercaya hewan hibrida hasil perkawinan singa betina yang tidak setia dengan pard.",
        "en": "Pliny writes that cheetahs were believed to be hybrids born of a promiscuous lioness and the pard."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pard-c03",
      "source_id": "pard-s1",
      "quote": "Pliny also states that the sexual passion between the pard and lioness is so violent that it enrages the male lion, who will often seek revenge on a lioness when smelling the pard.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Plinius, gairah antara pard dan singa betina begitu hebat sehingga membuat singa jantan murka dan membalas dendam bila mencium bau pard pada singa betina.",
        "en": "Pliny also says the passion between pard and lioness is so violent that it enrages the male lion, who takes revenge on the lioness when he smells the pard on her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pard-c04",
      "source_id": "pard-s1",
      "quote": "In the seventh-century book Etymologies, Isidore of Seville describes their coats as being mottled like a giraffe's. He goes on to describe them as being \"headlong for blood\" by being capable of killing their prey in a single leap.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Etymologiae abad ke-7, Isidorus dari Sevilla menggambarkan bulunya berbintik seperti jerapah dan menyebutnya haus darah karena dapat membunuh mangsa dalam satu lompatan.",
        "en": "In the 7th-century Etymologies, Isidore of Seville describes their coats as mottled like a giraffe's and calls them 'headlong for blood', able to kill prey in one leap."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pard-c05",
      "source_id": "pard-s1",
      "quote": "It was not until the 13th century that pards acquired their mythical reputation for being bloodthirsty and almost demonic creatures, primarily thanks to the MS Bodley 764 Bestiary. In this source, their spots were said to symbolize sins, the devil, or even the variety of vices in mankind.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Baru pada abad ke-13 pard mendapat reputasi mitis sebagai makhluk haus darah yang hampir iblis, terutama berkat Bestiari MS Bodley 764, yang menyebut bintik-bintiknya melambangkan dosa, iblis, atau aneka kebejatan manusia.",
        "en": "Only in the 13th century did pards gain their mythic reputation as bloodthirsty, almost demonic creatures, chiefly through the MS Bodley 764 Bestiary, in which their spots symbolised sins, the devil or the many vices of mankind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pard-c06",
      "source_id": "pard-s2",
      "quote": "El pardus es un animal de los bestiarios medievales. Fueron felinos con pelaje manchado y eran extremadamente rápidos (lo que recuerda a un guepardo). Se creía que se apareaban con los leones para producir leopardos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pardus adalah hewan bestiari abad pertengahan, kucing besar berbulu bintik yang sangat cepat, yang dipercaya kawin dengan singa untuk menghasilkan leopard.",
        "en": "The pardus is an animal of medieval bestiaries, a spotted, extremely fast feline believed to mate with lions to produce leopards."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Picolaton adalah burung khayalan dari Franche-Comté yang dipakai menakuti anak malas, karena konon mematuk tumit dan bokong mereka.",
    "en": "The picolaton is an imaginary bird of Franche-Comté used to scare lazy children, which supposedly nips their heels and buttocks.",

    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Picolaton, juga disebut pique-au-mollet atau cacalambri, adalah burung khayalan dalam cerita rakyat Franche-Comté. Anak malas ditakuti bahwa picolaton akan mematuk tumit dan bokong mereka bila tidak bergegas, sehingga ia mirip momok penakut anak. Anak yang menjengkelkan dijanjikan melihat sarang luar biasa burung itu di semak berduri sekitar 1 April, asalkan mereka patuh.",
      "en": "The picolaton, also called pique-au-mollet or cacalambri, is an imaginary bird of the folklore of Franche-Comté. Lazy children are warned that it will nip their heels and buttocks if they dawdle, making it akin to the bogeyman. Irritating children are promised a look at its incredible nest in the thorn bushes around April Fool's Day, if they behave.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "picolaton-c03",

    {
      "id": "picolaton-c01",
      "source_id": "picolaton-s1",
      "quote": "The picolaton (also known as the pique-au-mollet, quiperlibresson and cacalambri) is an imaginary bird present in the folklore of Franche-Comté.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Picolaton (juga disebut pique-au-mollet, quiperlibresson, dan cacalambri) adalah burung khayalan dalam cerita rakyat Franche-Comté.",
        "en": "The picolaton (also called pique-au-mollet, quiperlibresson and cacalambri) is an imaginary bird of the folklore of Franche-Comté."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "picolaton-c02",
      "source_id": "picolaton-s1",
      "quote": "It is best known as a creature used to threaten lazy children, by saying that the picolaton would nip their heels and buttocks if they did not hurry. It is thus akin to the bogeyman.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia terutama dikenal sebagai makhluk untuk menakuti anak malas: picolaton akan mematuk tumit dan bokong mereka bila tidak bergegas, sehingga ia mirip momok penakut anak.",
        "en": "It is best known as a creature used to threaten lazy children, who are told the picolaton will nip their heels and buttocks if they do not hurry; it is thus akin to the bogeyman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "picolaton-c03",
      "source_id": "picolaton-s1",
      "quote": "Particularly irritating children are promised a visit to the incredible nest of the fabulous bird around April Fool's Day, provided they are very good and obedient. It supposedly nests in thorn bushes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak yang menjengkelkan dijanjikan kunjungan ke sarang luar biasa burung itu sekitar 1 April bila mereka sangat baik dan patuh; burung itu konon bersarang di semak berduri.",
        "en": "Especially irritating children are promised a visit to the bird's incredible nest around April Fool's Day if they are very good; it supposedly nests in thorn bushes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "picolaton-c04",
      "source_id": "picolaton-s2",
      "quote": "পিকোলাটন (এটি পিক-ও-মলে, কুইপারলিব্রেসন এবং কাকালাম্ব্রি নামেও পরিচিত) ফ্রঁশ-কমতে অঞ্চলের লোকবিশ্বাসে বিদ্যমান একটি কাল্পনিক পাখি।",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Bengali juga menyebut picolaton sebagai burung khayalan dalam kepercayaan rakyat Franche-Comté.",
        "en": "The Bengali article likewise calls the picolaton an imaginary bird of Franche-Comté folk belief."
```

```json
    "en": "Rodea or Rhodeia is one of the Oceanids, daughters of Oceanus and Tethys in Greek mythology, and a playmate of Persephone.",

    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Yunani, Rodea adalah salah satu Okeanid yang disebut Hesiodos, putri Titan Okeanos dan Tethys yang punya hingga 3.000 saudari. Ia salah satu teman bermain Persefone, putri Demeter. Namanya mungkin variasi ejaan Rodo, nimfa yang menjadi asal nama pulau Rhodes.",
      "en": "In Greek mythology Rodea is one of the Oceanids named by Hesiod, a daughter of the Titans Oceanus and Tethys with up to 3,000 sisters. She was one of the playmates of Persephone, daughter of Demeter. Her name may be a variant of Rodo, the nymph who gave Rhodes its name.",

      "source_id": "rodea-s1",
      "quote": "En la mitología griega, Rodea (en griego antiguo Ρόδεια) era una de las oceánides, hijas de Océano y Tetis,​ y una de las compañeras de juegos de Perséfone.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Yunani, Rodea (Ρόδεια, Rhodeia) adalah salah satu Okeanid, putri Okeanos dan Tethys, dan salah satu teman bermain Persefone.",
        "en": "In Greek mythology Rodea (Ρόδεια, Rhodeia) was one of the Oceanids, daughters of Oceanus and Tethys, and one of Persephone's playmates."
      }

    {
      "id": "rodea-c02",
      "source_id": "rodea-s1",
      "quote": "Pudiera ser una variante gráfica de Rodo (Ῥόδος), la ninfa epónima de Rodas, ya que en otra fuente a Rodo se la imagina como una de las oceánides.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya mungkin variasi ejaan Rodo (Rhodos), nimfa yang menjadi asal nama pulau Rhodes, karena di sumber lain Rodo juga dianggap Okeanid.",
        "en": "The name may be a spelling variant of Rodo (Rhodos), the nymph who gave Rhodes its name, since another source also counts Rodo among the Oceanids."
      }

    {
      "id": "rodea-c03",
      "source_id": "rodea-s2",
      "quote": "En la mitologia grega, Rodea (en grec antic Ρόδεια) era la major de les oceànides, una nimfa, filla d'Oceà i de Tetis, que Hesíode cita a la seva llista d'oceànides.",
      "locator": null,

    {
      "id": "rodea-c04",
      "source_id": "rodea-s2",
      "quote": "Els seus pares van ser els titans Oceà i Tetis, i va tenir moltes germanes, segons Hesíode fins a 3.000",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang tuanya adalah Titan Okeanos dan Tethys, dan menurut Hesiodos ia punya hingga 3.000 saudari.",
        "en": "Her parents were the Titans Oceanus and Tethys, and according to Hesiod she had up to 3,000 sisters."

      "source_id": "rodea-s3",
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Redon atau Rodon adalah dewa laut dan pelindung pelaut dalam mitologi Iliria, digambarkan bertopi petasos pada koin kuno.",
    "en": "Redon or Rodon is the sea god and protector of sailors in Illyrian mythology, shown wearing a petasos on ancient coins.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "rodon-c01",
      "rodon-c04",
      "rodon-c05"
    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Redon atau Rodon adalah dewa laut dalam mitologi Iliria, padanan Poseidon dan Neptunus. Pemujaannya mungkin menyebar sejak abad ke-2 SM dan bertahan setelah penaklukan Romawi. Ia diduga dewa pelaut dan pejalan, dengan topi petasos seperti Hermes. Tanjung Rodon dekat Durrës di Albania dinamai menurut dewa ini.",
      "en": "Redon or Rodon is the sea god of Illyrian mythology, counterpart of Poseidon and Neptune. His cult probably spread from the 2nd century BC and survived the Roman conquest. He is thought to have protected sailors and travellers and wears a petasos like Hermes. Cape Rodon near Durrës in Albania is named after him.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "rodon-c01",
        "rodon-c02",
        "rodon-c03",
        "rodon-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hasan Ceka pada 1970-an mengidentifikasi kepala bertopi petasos pada koin Lissos sebagai Redon; potretnya disertai perahu Iliria dan lumba-lumba.",
      "en": "In the 1970s Hasan Ceka identified the petasos-wearing head on Lissus coins as Redon; his portrait is accompanied by an Illyrian ship and a dolphin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "rodon-c05",
        "rodon-c06"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "rodon-c01",
      "source_id": "rodon-s1",
      "quote": "Rodon, nella mitologia illirica era il dio del mare. Si può considerare come l'equivalente di Poseidone greco, Nethuns etrusco e Nettuno romano.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rodon dalam mitologi Iliria adalah dewa laut, padanan Poseidon Yunani, Nethuns Etruria, dan Neptunus Romawi.",
        "en": "In Illyrian mythology Rodon was the god of the sea, equivalent to the Greek Poseidon, Etruscan Nethuns and Roman Neptune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodon-c02",
      "source_id": "rodon-s1",
      "quote": "Nell'Albania di oggi si trova, vicino a Durazzo, il Capo Rodoni (Kepi i Rodonit), così nominato per onorare il Dio del mare, dei terremoti e dei cavalli.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Albania sekarang, dekat Durrës, ada Tanjung Rodon (Kepi i Rodonit) yang dinamai untuk menghormati dewa laut, gempa bumi, dan kuda itu.",
        "en": "In modern Albania, near Durrës, Cape Rodon (Kepi i Rodonit) is named in honour of the god of the sea, earthquakes and horses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodon-c03",
      "source_id": "rodon-s2",
      "quote": "Redon az ókori illírek egyik istene volt, kultusza feltételezhetően az i. e. 2. században terjedt el előbb Dél-Illíriában, majd az észak-illíriai területeken is, és még századokig fennmaradt a római hódítást követően is.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Hungaria, Redon adalah dewa Iliria kuno yang pemujaannya mungkin menyebar sejak abad ke-2 SM di Iliria selatan lalu utara, dan bertahan berabad-abad setelah penaklukan Romawi.",
        "en": "According to the Hungarian article, Redon was an ancient Illyrian god whose cult probably spread from the 2nd century BC in southern then northern Illyria, surviving for centuries after the Roman conquest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodon-c04",
      "source_id": "rodon-s2",
      "quote": "Feltételezések szerint a hajósok és az utazók, a tenger istene volt, jellemző attribútuma a petaszosz néven ismert, a görög Hermészéhez hasonló fejfedő.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia diduga dewa para pelaut, pejalan, dan laut, dengan atribut khas topi petasos seperti milik Hermes Yunani.",
        "en": "He is thought to have been the god of sailors, travellers and the sea, his characteristic attribute a petasos hat like that of the Greek Hermes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodon-c05",
      "source_id": "rodon-s2",
      "quote": "Hasan Ceka, az illír éremkincs monográfusa az 1970-es években rámutatott, hogy az illírek tengeristenéről, Redonról van szó.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1970-an Hasan Ceka, penulis monografi koin Iliria, menunjukkan bahwa kepala bertopi petasos pada koin Lissos adalah Redon, dewa laut Iliria.",
        "en": "In the 1970s Hasan Ceka, author of the monograph on Illyrian coins, showed that the petasos-wearing head on the Lissus coins is Redon, the Illyrian sea god."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodon-c06",
      "source_id": "rodon-s3",
      "quote": "Portreti shoqërohet me figurën e lembes ilire e me delfinin si atribute te hyjnisë.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Albania, potretnya disertai gambar perahu lembus Iliria dan lumba-lumba sebagai lambang dewa itu.",
        "en": "According to the Albanian article, his portrait is accompanied by the Illyrian lembus ship and a dolphin as the god's attributes."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
```

```json
    "en": "The dragon of Rhodes is a legendary dragon that terrorised Rhodes in the 1340s until killed by the Hospitaller knight Dieudonné de Gozon.",

    {
      "id": "Naga Rhodes menurut legenda meneror lembah Soudourli di kaki gunung Santo Stefanus pada 1340-an, masa kekuasaan Ordo Hospitaller. Korbannya begitu banyak sehingga Grand Master Hélion de Villeneuve melarang para ksatria melawannya. Menurut Bosio naga itu sebesar kuda, berkepala ular, bertelinga bagal bersisik, dan bergigi tajam.",
      "en": "The dragon of Rhodes is said to have terrorised the Soudourli valley at the foot of Mount Saint Stephen in the 1340s, under the Knights Hospitaller. It claimed so many victims that Grand Master Hélion de Villeneuve forbade the knights to fight it. Bosio describes it as the size of a horse, with a serpent's head, scaly mule's ears and sharp teeth.",

      "id": "Ksatria Dieudonné de Gozon melanggar larangan itu: ia berlatih dengan naga kayu berengsel, kembali sekitar 1342 bersama dua anjing besar, membunuh naga itu, dan memasang kepalanya di benteng. Legenda serupa sudah ada di Kos sejak 1420, dan sebagian sarjana menduga kisah ini berawal dari hewan nyata.",
      "en": "The knight Dieudonné de Gozon defied the ban: he trained with an articulated wooden dragon, returned around 1342 with two mastiffs, killed the dragon and set its head on the walls. A similar legend existed on Kos from 1420, and some scholars think the story stems from a real animal.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c01",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s1",
      "quote": "Le dragon de l'île de Rhodes est un animal mythique qui, d'après la légende, semait la terreur dans la « vallée de Soudourli », au pied du mont Saint-Étienne (« Agios Stephanos », actuellement « Monte Smith »).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga pulau Rhodes adalah hewan mitos yang menurut legenda meneror \"lembah Soudourli\" di kaki gunung Santo Stefanus (Agios Stephanos, kini Monte Smith).",
        "en": "The dragon of Rhodes is a mythical animal that, according to legend, terrorised the 'Soudourli valley' at the foot of Mount Saint Stephen (Agios Stephanos, now Monte Smith)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c02",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s1",
      "quote": "Ce dragon faisait tant de victimes que Hélion de Villeneuve, le 26e grand maître des Hospitaliers de l'ordre de Saint-Jean de Jérusalem, avait interdit à ses chevaliers de se mesurer à lui.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga itu memakan begitu banyak korban sehingga Hélion de Villeneuve, Grand Master ke-26 Ordo Hospitaller Santo Yohanes, melarang para ksatrianya melawannya.",
        "en": "The dragon claimed so many victims that Hélion de Villeneuve, 26th Grand Master of the Knights Hospitaller of St John, forbade his knights to fight it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c03",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s1",
      "quote": "le Dragon estoit de la grosseur d'un cheval moyen, la teste de serpent, les oreilles de mulet couvertes de peau fort dure et escailles, les dents fort aigus",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda Bosio, naga itu sebesar kuda sedang, berkepala ular, bertelinga bagal yang berkulit sangat keras dan bersisik, dan bergigi sangat tajam.",
        "en": "In Bosio's legend the dragon was the size of a medium horse, with a serpent's head, mule's ears covered in very hard, scaly skin, and very sharp teeth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c04",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s1",
      "quote": "Gozon serait rentré dans son fief pour s'entraîner grâce à un dragon articulé en bois, puis, revenu à Rhodes, il l'aurait affronté vers 1342 avec deux molosses, l'aurait tué et aurait planté sa tête sur les fortifications de la ville.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ksatria Dieudonné de Gozon pulang ke tanahnya untuk berlatih dengan naga kayu berengsel, lalu kembali ke Rhodes sekitar 1342 bersama dua anjing besar, membunuh naga itu, dan memasang kepalanya di benteng kota.",
        "en": "The knight Dieudonné de Gozon went home to train with an articulated wooden dragon, then returned to Rhodes around 1342 with two mastiffs, killed the dragon and set its head on the city walls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c05",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s1",
      "quote": "d'autres font remarquer qu'il existait à Kos, autre possession des chevaliers de Saint-Jean, une légende semblable dès 1420 et que dans sa plus ancienne forme connue, datée de 1521, la légende de Rhodes a pour héros un chevalier anonyme",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian penulis mencatat legenda serupa sudah ada di Kos sejak 1420, dan versi tertua legenda Rhodes yang diketahui, dari 1521, berpahlawan seorang ksatria tanpa nama.",
        "en": "Others note that a similar legend existed on Kos from 1420, and that the oldest known form of the Rhodes legend, from 1521, has an anonymous knight as hero."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c06",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s2",
      "quote": "Учёные исследуют данную легенду на протяжении нескольких сотен лет; некоторые полагают, что за данной историей, особенно с учётом описанного в ней небольшого размера дракона, может стоять убийство какого-то реального существа",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, para sarjana meneliti legenda ini selama ratusan tahun, dan sebagian menduga, mengingat ukuran naga yang kecil, kisah ini berawal dari terbunuhnya hewan nyata.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, scholars have studied the legend for centuries, and some think that, given the dragon's small size, it may stem from the killing of a real animal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rodos-jdahas-c07",
      "source_id": "rodos-jdahas-s3",
      "quote": "Rodos əjdahası — əfsanəyə görə 1340-cı illərdə, səlibçilərin hökmranlığı dövründə, Rodos adasının əhalisinə divan tutan və Müqəddəs Stefan dağının (Monte Smit) ətəyində yaşayan kiçik ölçülərə malik əjdaha.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Azerbaijan menyebutnya naga kecil yang pada 1340-an, masa kekuasaan tentara salib, meneror penduduk Rhodes dari kaki gunung Santo Stefanus.",
        "en": "The Azerbaijani article calls it a small dragon that in the 1340s, under crusader rule, terrorised the people of Rhodes from the foot of Mount Saint Stephen."
      }
    }
```

```json
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Rompo adalah binatang campuran dari India dan Afrika, berkepala terwelu dan bertelinga manusia, yang menggali kuburan untuk memakan mayat.",
    "en": "The Rompo is a hybrid beast of India and Africa, with a hare's head and human ears, that digs up graves to eat corpses.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "rompo-c01",
      "rompo-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Rompo adalah binatang mitos yang konon ada di India dan Afrika. Ia berkepala terwelu, bertelinga manusia, bersurai, bertubuh ramping seperti kerangka, berekor berumbai, berkaki depan luak, dan berkaki belakang beruang. Ia memakan mayat manusia dari kuburan, sehingga dijuluki \"Pemakan Manusia\".",
      "en": "The Rompo is a mythological beast said to live in India and Africa. It has a hare's head, human ears, a mane, a slender, skeletal body, a tufted tail, a badger's forefeet and a bear's hind feet. It eats human corpses dug from graves, earning the name 'Man-Eater'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "rompo-c01",
        "rompo-c02",
        "rompo-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia mengitari mayat beberapa kali sebelum memakannya seolah takut, dan konon bersenandung pelan saat makan. Legenda ini mungkin terilhami perilaku hyena.",
      "en": "It circles a corpse several times before eating as if afraid, and is said to hum softly while it eats. The legend may have been inspired by hyenas.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "rompo-c04"
      ]
    }

      "id": "rompo-c01",
      "source_id": "rompo-s1",
      "quote": "The Rompo is a mythological beast said to have been found in India and Africa. It is described as having the head and mouth of a hare, the ears of a human, a mane, a slender body approximately three feet in length, a long tail ending in a hairy brush, the front feet of a badger, and the rear feet of a bear.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rompo adalah binatang mitos yang konon ada di India dan Afrika, berkepala dan bermulut terwelu, bertelinga manusia, bersurai, bertubuh ramping sekitar tiga kaki, berekor panjang dengan ujung berumbai, berkaki depan luak, dan berkaki belakang beruang.",
        "en": "The Rompo is a mythological beast said to live in India and Africa, with a hare's head and mouth, human ears, a mane, a slender body about three feet long, a long tail ending in a hairy brush, a badger's forefeet and a bear's hind feet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rompo-c02",
      "source_id": "rompo-s1",
      "quote": "It feeds on human corpses, which it obtains by digging up graves, and is termed the \"Man-Eater\" due to this habit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia memakan mayat manusia yang diperolehnya dengan menggali kuburan, sehingga dijuluki \"Pemakan Manusia\".",
        "en": "It feeds on human corpses dug from graves, and is called the 'Man-Eater' for this habit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rompo-c03",
      "source_id": "rompo-s1",
      "quote": "After discovering a body, instead of immediately devouring it, it circles around it several times as if afraid to seize it. The legend of the Rompo may have been inspired by the ecology and behavior of hyenas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah menemukan mayat, ia tidak langsung memakannya tetapi mengitarinya beberapa kali seolah takut; legenda ini mungkin terilhami perilaku hyena.",
        "en": "On finding a body it does not devour it at once but circles it several times as if afraid; the legend may have been inspired by hyenas' behaviour."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rompo-c04",
      "source_id": "rompo-s2",
      "quote": "Sólo se alimenta de cadáveres humanos y se dice que canturrea en voz baja mientras come.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia hanya makan mayat manusia dan konon bersenandung pelan saat makan.",
        "en": "It feeds only on human corpses and is said to hum softly while eating."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rompo-c05",
      "source_id": "rompo-s3",
      "quote": "Rompo egy mitológiai bestia, teste csontvázszerű, feje egy nyúlé, emberi fülekkel.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Hungaria, tubuh Rompo seperti kerangka, berkepala kelinci dan bertelinga manusia.",
        "en": "According to the Hungarian article, the Rompo has a skeletal body and a hare's head with human ears."
      }
```

```json
    "en": "Šarac is the winged piebald horse of Prince Marko in Serbian epic and South Slavic folklore, able to speak and drink wine.",

    {
      "id": "Šarac adalah kuda Pangeran Marko, pahlawan puisi epik Serbia dan cerita rakyat Slavia Selatan. Ia kuda belang yang warnanya mirip sapi, bersayap, dan secepat peri. Ia memahami dan berbicara bahasa Serbia, Turki, dan Yunani, minum anggur bersama tuannya, menasihatinya, menolongnya di medan perang, dan merasakan firasat kematiannya.",
      "en": "Šarac is the horse of Prince Marko, hero of Serbian epic poetry and South Slavic folklore. He is a piebald horse coloured like a cow, winged and as fast as a fairy. He understands and speaks Serbian, Turkish and Greek, drinks wine with his master, advises him, helps him in battle and foresees his death.",

        "sarac-c02",
        "sarac-c03",
        "sarac-c06"

    {
      "id": "Marko mendapatkannya dari samovila atau membelinya sebagai anak kuda bungkuk yang tak bergeser saat ekornya ditarik, lalu menyembuhkannya dan mengajarinya minum anggur. Menurut tradisi lisan, Marko sendiri membunuh Šarac agar tidak dipakai orang Turki untuk kerja kasar.",
      "en": "Marko got him from the samovilas, or bought him as a hunchbacked foal that would not budge when he pulled its tail, then healed it and taught it to drink wine. Oral tradition says Marko himself killed Šarac so the Turks would not use him for menial work.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "sarac-c01",
      "source_id": "sarac-s1",
      "quote": "Šarac (en serbio: Шарац; Šarec, en macedonio: Шарец) era el caballo del Príncipe Marko, un héroe de la poesía épica serbia y del folclore de los eslavos meridionales.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Šarac (bahasa Serbia: Шарац; bahasa Makedonia: Šarec) adalah kuda Pangeran Marko, pahlawan puisi epik Serbia dan cerita rakyat Slavia Selatan.",
        "en": "Šarac (Serbian Шарац; Macedonian Šarec) was the horse of Prince Marko, a hero of Serbian epic poetry and South Slavic folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c02",
      "source_id": "sarac-s1",
      "quote": "Es descrito como un caballo moteado, cuyo color se asemeja a una vaca, que tenía características milagrosas. Era un caballo alado, rápido como un hada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai kuda belang yang warnanya mirip sapi, punya sifat ajaib, bersayap, dan secepat peri.",
        "en": "It is described as a piebald horse coloured like a cow, with miraculous qualities, winged and as fast as a fairy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c03",
      "source_id": "sarac-s1",
      "quote": "Šarac entendía serbio, turco y griego (incluso hablaba estos idiomas; bebía vino con su amo, le aconsejaba y venía en su ayuda en el campo de batalla.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Šarac memahami bahkan berbicara bahasa Serbia, Turki, dan Yunani, minum anggur bersama tuannya, memberinya nasihat, dan menolongnya di medan perang.",
        "en": "Šarac understood and even spoke Serbian, Turkish and Greek, drank wine with his master, advised him and helped him in battle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c04",
      "source_id": "sarac-s1",
      "quote": "Según la tradición oral, Šarac murió a manos de Marko para que los turcos no lo usaran en trabajos domésticos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi lisan, Šarac dibunuh Marko sendiri agar tidak dipakai orang Turki untuk pekerjaan kasar.",
        "en": "According to oral tradition Šarac was killed by Marko so the Turks would not use him for menial work."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c05",
      "source_id": "sarac-s2",
      "quote": "Според народните преданија, тој него го добил од самовилите или го купил од кираџиите како грбаво ждребе.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita rakyat Makedonia, Marko mendapatkannya dari para samovila atau membelinya dari penyewa kuda sebagai anak kuda yang bungkuk.",
        "en": "According to Macedonian folk tradition Marko got him from the samovilas or bought him from carters as a hunchbacked foal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c06",
      "source_id": "sarac-s2",
      "quote": "Тој ја предосетува и смртта на својот господар.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga merasakan firasat kematian tuannya.",
        "en": "He also foresees his master's death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c07",
      "source_id": "sarac-s3",
      "quote": "Шарац (такође Шарин, Шаро или Шарко) је коњ легендарног јунака Марка Краљевића у народном предању уз Ждрала Милоша Обилића и Јабучила, крилатог коња Војводе Момчила.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Serbia, Šarac (juga Šarin, Šaro, atau Šarko) adalah kuda pahlawan Marko Kraljević dalam tradisi rakyat, bersama Ždral milik Miloš Obilić dan Jabučilo, kuda bersayap Vojvoda Momčilo.",
        "en": "According to the Serbian article, Šarac (also Šarin, Šaro or Šarko) is the horse of the hero Marko Kraljević in folk tradition, alongside Miloš Obilić's Ždral and Jabučilo, the winged horse of Vojvoda Momčilo."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sarac-c08",
      "source_id": "sarac-s3",
      "quote": "Узео га је за реп и покушао окретати око себе, али се ждребе није померало са места, па га је купио, излечио и научио да пије вино.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam satu kisah Marko memegang ekor anak kuda itu dan mencoba memutarnya, tetapi anak kuda itu tak bergeser, sehingga Marko membelinya, menyembuhkannya, dan mengajarinya minum anggur.",
        "en": "In one tale Marko grabbed the foal by the tail and tried to swing it round, but it did not budge, so he bought it, healed it and taught it to drink wine."
      }
```

```json
    "id": "Scitalis adalah ular bestiari abad pertengahan yang corak punggungnya memukau mangsa dan tubuhnya begitu panas sehingga berganti kulit di musim dingin.",
    "en": "The Scitalis is a serpent of medieval bestiaries whose dazzling back markings stun its prey and whose body is so hot it sheds its skin in winter.",
    "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Scitalis atau Scytale adalah ular dalam bestiari abad pertengahan seperti Aberdeen Bestiary. Corak punggungnya begitu indah sehingga orang yang melihatnya terpana dan melambat; karena merayap pelan dan tak dapat mengejar mangsa, ia memakai penampilannya untuk memikat. Tubuhnya begitu panas sehingga ia berganti kulit bahkan di musim dingin, seperti sudah ditulis Lucanus dalam Pharsalia.",
      "en": "The Scitalis or Scytale is a serpent of medieval bestiaries such as the Aberdeen Bestiary. Its back markings are so beautiful that viewers are stunned and slowed; since it creeps slowly and cannot chase prey, it uses its looks to ensnare. Its body is so hot it sheds its skin even in winter, as Lucan already wrote in the Pharsalia.",

      "id": "scitalis-c01",
      "source_id": "scitalis-s1",
      "quote": "The Scitalis or Scytale is a serpent from Medieval bestiaries, such as the Aberdeen Bestiary, supposed to have such marvelous markings on its back that its appearance would stun the viewer, slowing the person down so that they could be caught.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Scitalis atau Scytale adalah ular dalam bestiari abad pertengahan seperti Aberdeen Bestiary, yang punggungnya bercorak begitu indah sehingga orang yang melihatnya terpana dan melambat hingga dapat ditangkap.",

      }
    },
    {
      "id": "scitalis-c02",
      "source_id": "scitalis-s1",
      "quote": "Its bodily heat was so great that it shed its skin even in the winter.",

      "quote": "Lucan wrote in the 1st century AD, in his Pharsalia, book 9, verse 841-842, that \"Sole of all serpents Scytale to shed / In vernal frosts his slough...\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",

        "en": "Lucan wrote in the 1st century AD, in Pharsalia book 9, lines 841-842, that the Scytale alone of all serpents sheds its slough in the spring frosts."

    },
    {
      "id": "scitalis-c04",

    {
      "id": "scitalis-c05",
      "source_id": "scitalis-s2",
      "quote": "La scitale (in latino scitalis o scytale, probabilmente da scitus, \"grazioso, bello\") è un serpente leggendario descritto nei bestiari medievali.",

      "id": "scitalis-c06",
      "source_id": "scitalis-s2",
      "quote": "La scitale compare nel novero degli animali, reali e leggendari, che formano la cornice del portale della chiesa ne Il nome della rosa, romanzo di Umberto Eco.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Scitale termasuk hewan nyata dan legendaris yang menghiasi bingkai portal gereja dalam novel Il nome della rosa karya Umberto Eco.",
        "en": "The scitale is among the real and legendary animals framing the church portal in Umberto Eco's novel Il nome della rosa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "scitalis-c07",
      "source_id": "scitalis-s3",
      "quote": "স্কিটালিস অথবা স্কাইটেল একটি পৌরাণিক সাপ। এটি মধ্যযুগীয় বেস্টিয়ারি যেমন অ্যাবারডিন বেস্টিয়ারি তে উল্লেখিত হয়েছে।",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Bengali juga menyebutnya ular mitos yang tercatat dalam bestiari abad pertengahan seperti Aberdeen Bestiary.",
        "en": "The Bengali article likewise calls it a mythical serpent recorded in medieval bestiaries such as the Aberdeen Bestiary."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Trenti adalah goblin hutan jahil dalam mitologi Cantabria, berwajah hitam dan bermata hijau, yang suka menarik rok perempuan tetapi diam-diam menolong orang.",
    "en": "The Trenti is a mischievous forest goblin of Cantabrian mythology, black-faced and green-eyed, that pulls women's skirts but secretly helps people.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "trenti-c01",
      "trenti-c02",
      "trenti-c03",
      "trenti-c04"
    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Trenti adalah goblin hutan dalam mitologi Cantabria yang menjengkelkan tetapi tidak jahat. Ia sulit dilihat karena mirip jamur, daun, dan lumut, wajahnya hitam legam, dan matanya hijau. Ia suka melompat dari semak untuk menakuti pejalan dan menarik rok perempuan.",
      "en": "The Trenti is a forest goblin of Cantabrian mythology, annoying but not malicious. It is hard to see because it looks like mushrooms, leaves and moss, with an all-black face and green eyes. It likes jumping out of bushes to scare walkers and pulling women's skirts.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "trenti-c01",
        "trenti-c02",
        "trenti-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Diam-diam ia juga menolong manusia, terutama anak-anak, gembala yang kehilangan ternak setelah badai atau ulah Ojáncanu, dan nenek-nenek yang lemah. Ia makan jagung dan buah blackthorn tetapi tidak pernah minum air, yang beracun baginya; musim dingin ia tidur di lubang tanah dan musim panas di bawah pohon.",
      "en": "It also secretly helps people, especially children, shepherds whose flocks were scattered by storms or the Ojáncanu, and frail old women. It eats corncobs and sloes but never drinks water, which poisons it; in winter it sleeps in sinkholes and in summer under trees.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "trenti-c04",

      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "trenti-c01",
      "source_id": "trenti-s1",
      "quote": "The Trenti is an imp-like creature that comes from the Cantabrian mythology, northern Spain. This annoying but not malicious goblin is very difficult to see because it lives deep in the forest and resembles mushrooms, leaves, and moss.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Trenti adalah makhluk mirip imp dari mitologi Cantabria di Spanyol utara, goblin yang menjengkelkan tetapi tidak jahat, sulit dilihat karena tinggal jauh di dalam hutan dan mirip jamur, daun, dan lumut.",
        "en": "The Trenti is an imp-like creature of Cantabrian mythology in northern Spain, an annoying but not malicious goblin that is hard to see because it lives deep in the forest and looks like mushrooms, leaves and moss."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "trenti-c02",
      "source_id": "trenti-s1",
      "quote": "Its most distinguishing features are a completely black face with green eyes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cirinya yang paling khas adalah wajah hitam legam dengan mata hijau.",
        "en": "Its most distinctive features are an all-black face and green eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "trenti-c03",
      "source_id": "trenti-s1",
      "quote": "This creature tends to stay away from humans but it does like to jump out of the bushes and scare innocent walkers. Its favorite prank is to pull women's skirts.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia cenderung menjauhi manusia, tetapi suka melompat dari semak untuk menakuti pejalan, dan lelucon kesukaannya menarik rok perempuan.",
        "en": "It tends to avoid people but likes jumping out of bushes to scare walkers, and its favourite prank is pulling women's skirts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "trenti-c04",
      "source_id": "trenti-s2",
      "quote": "Aunque son bromistas también pueden ayudar al hombre sin que estos lo sepan, sintiendo especial predilección por los niños. Auxilian al pastor a encontrar su ganado tras la tormenta o las maldades del Ojáncanu y ayudan a las viejas que no pueden valerse por sí mismas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski jahil, mereka juga diam-diam menolong manusia, terutama anak-anak, membantu gembala menemukan ternak setelah badai atau ulah Ojáncanu, dan menolong nenek-nenek yang lemah.",
        "en": "Though pranksters, they also secretly help people, especially children, helping shepherds find livestock after storms or the Ojáncanu's mischief, and aiding old women who cannot fend for themselves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "trenti-c05",
      "source_id": "trenti-s2",
      "quote": "Se alimenta de panojas y endrinas, pero jamás bebe agua, pues es veneno para él.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia makan jagung dan buah blackthorn, tetapi tidak pernah minum air karena air racun baginya.",
        "en": "It eats corncobs and sloes but never drinks water, which is poison to it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "trenti-c06",
      "source_id": "trenti-s3",
      "quote": "Vintre ili protektite dormas en dolinoj kaj somere ili freŝe dormas sub arboj.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel Esperanto, pada musim dingin mereka tidur terlindung di lubang-lubang tanah (dolina) dan pada musim panas di bawah pohon yang sejuk.",
        "en": "According to the Esperanto article, in winter they sleep sheltered in sinkholes and in summer under cool trees."
      }
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Zuzidun Txakurra adalah roh anjing bercahaya dalam mitologi Basque yang muncul setelah kematian sebagai tanda jiwa yang terkutuk dan menuntut utangnya dibayar.",
    "en": "Zuzidun Txakurra is a glowing dog spirit of Basque mythology that appears after a death as a sign of a cursed soul seeking to settle its debts.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "zuzidun-txakurra-c01",
      "zuzidun-txakurra-c02",
      "zuzidun-txakurra-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Zuzidun Txakurra, \"anjing obor\", adalah roh mitologi Basque yang muncul sebagai anjing dengan obor menyala di moncongnya. Bila ia muncul setelah seseorang meninggal, jiwa orang itu terkutuk; arwah seperti ini menuntut ganti rugi atas kesalahan semasa hidup. Legenda ini khas Barakaldo, dan Resurrección María de Azkue mencatat bahwa legenda ini hampir punah.",
      "en": "Zuzidun Txakurra, 'the dog of the torch', is a Basque spirit that appears as a dog with a burning torch in its muzzle. If it appears after someone's death, that person's soul is cursed; such souls demand reparation for wrongs done in life. The legend belongs to Barakaldo, and Resurrección María de Azkue noted it was nearly extinct.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c01",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c02",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c03",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Barakaldo anjing itu besar, hitam, dengan api di mata dan rahangnya, dan selalu lari lalu lenyap ke dalam air, bahkan ember kecil. Seorang warga menjebaknya dengan lauburu yang digambar mengelilingi rumah tanpa ada air; anjing itu mengaku arwah tetangga yang mati sebelum mengembalikan barang curian, dan baru tenang setelah warga itu berjanji membayar utangnya.",
      "en": "In Barakaldo the dog was huge and black with fire in its eyes and jaws, always fleeing and vanishing into water, even a small bucket. One villager trapped it with a lauburu drawn around a house with no water; the dog confessed to being the spirit of a neighbour who died before returning stolen goods, and found peace when the villager promised to pay the debt.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c04",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c05",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c06",
        "zuzidun-txakurra-c07"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c01",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s2",
      "quote": "Zuzidun Txakurra ('el gos de la teia', del basc zuzidun 'proveït, dotat de torxes' i txakurra 'el gos'), conegut en castellà com El perro de la tea, és un esperit propi de la mitologia basca que s'apareix en forma de gos amb una teia encesa al musell.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zuzidun Txakurra (\"anjing obor\"), dalam bahasa Spanyol El perro de la tea, adalah roh mitologi Basque yang muncul berwujud anjing dengan obor menyala di moncongnya.",
        "en": "Zuzidun Txakurra ('the dog of the torch'), in Spanish El perro de la tea, is a spirit of Basque mythology that appears as a dog with a burning torch in its muzzle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c02",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s2",
      "quote": "Segons la llegenda, si Zuzidun Txakurra apareix després de la mort d'algú, és senyal que l'ànima del difunt està maleïda. Aquesta llegenda és pròpia de la zona de Barakaldo, tot i que Resurrección María de Azkue la va recollir a Otxandio, fent constar que estava a punt de desaparèixer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, bila Zuzidun Txakurra muncul setelah seseorang meninggal, itu tanda jiwa orang itu terkutuk; legenda ini khas daerah Barakaldo, meski Resurrección María de Azkue mencatatnya di Otxandio dan menyebut legenda ini hampir punah.",
        "en": "In legend, if Zuzidun Txakurra appears after someone's death, it is a sign that the dead person's soul is cursed; the legend belongs to the Barakaldo area, though Resurrección María de Azkue recorded it at Otxandio, noting it was about to disappear."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c03",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s1",
      "quote": "Celles-ci apparaissent aux vivants sous la forme d'un chien lumineux demandant réparation ou restitution pour le tort fait dans leur vie et/ou lors la célébration de messes pour leur âme.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Arwah itu muncul kepada orang hidup sebagai anjing bercahaya yang menuntut ganti rugi atas kesalahan semasa hidupnya atau misa bagi jiwanya.",
        "en": "These souls appear to the living as a glowing dog demanding reparation for wrongs done in life or Masses for their soul."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c04",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s1",
      "quote": "Immense, noir et avec du feu dans ses yeux et dans ses mâchoires. Beaucoup ont surpris le txakurra qui traînait autour des maisons, mais ils n'ont jamais pu l'attraper.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Barakaldo anjing itu digambarkan besar, hitam, dengan api di mata dan rahangnya; banyak yang melihatnya berkeliaran di sekitar rumah tetapi tak pernah bisa menangkapnya.",
        "en": "In Barakaldo it was described as huge and black with fire in its eyes and jaws; many saw it prowling around houses but none could catch it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c05",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s1",
      "quote": "Il a toujours fui jusqu'à ce qu'il plonge dans n'importe quel endroit avec de l'eau, un puits, un étang et même dans un petit seau, puis disparaissait.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia selalu lari lalu terjun ke tempat berair, entah sumur, kolam, atau ember kecil, dan menghilang.",
        "en": "It always fled until it plunged into any water, a well, a pond or even a small bucket, and vanished."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c06",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s1",
      "quote": "il dessina un lauburu autour de la maison, marquant le sol avec une houe. Puis il s'assura qu'il n'y ait pas de flaque d'eau ou de récipient contenant de l'eau, et attendit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang warga Barakaldo menggambar lauburu mengelilingi rumahnya dengan cangkul dan menyingkirkan semua genangan dan wadah air, lalu menunggu anjing itu.",
        "en": "A Barakaldo villager drew a lauburu around his house with a hoe, removed every puddle and container of water, and waited for the dog."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c07",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s1",
      "quote": "« Je suis l'esprit de ton voisin qui est mort sans retourner les biens que j'ai volés à ton père. Et je ne peux pas sortir autrement jusqu’à ce que quelqu’un paie ma dette. »",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Terkurung di dalam lauburu, anjing itu berkata: \"Aku arwah tetanggamu yang mati tanpa mengembalikan barang yang kucuri dari ayahmu, dan aku tak bisa keluar sampai ada yang membayar utangku.\"",
        "en": "Trapped inside the lauburu, the dog said: 'I am the spirit of your neighbour, who died without returning what I stole from your father, and I cannot leave until someone pays my debt.'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zuzidun-txakurra-c08",
      "source_id": "zuzidun-txakurra-s3",
      "quote": "Jasotako kondairen arabera, dirudienez, pertsona bat hil ondoren Zuzidun Txakurra agertzen bada hildakoaren arima madarikatua dagoen seinalea da.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Basque juga mencatat bahwa kemunculan Zuzidun Txakurra setelah kematian seseorang menandakan jiwa orang itu terkutuk.",
        "en": "The Basque article also records that Zuzidun Txakurra appearing after someone's death signals that the dead person's soul is cursed."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia fr, ca, dan eu; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ammuntadore adalah makhluk mitologi Sardinia yang menyerang orang tidur lewat mimpi buruk dan tekanan di dada, mirip incubus Romawi.",
    "en": "The ammuntadore is a creature of Sardinian mythology that attacks sleepers with nightmares and chest pressure, much like the Roman incubus.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ammuntadore-c01",
      "ammuntadore-c04",
      "ammuntadore-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ammuntadòre, dari kata Sardinia untuk \"bermimpi buruk\", adalah makhluk yang menyerang orang dalam tidur lewat mimpi buruk. Korbannya merasakan tekanan kuat di dada, melihat penglihatan mengerikan, dan kadang mati sesak napas. Orang percaya rasa iri orang lainlah yang mendatangkannya.",
      "en": "The ammuntadòre, from the Sardinian word for 'having nightmares', is a creature that attacks people in their sleep through nightmares. Its victims feel strong pressure on the chest, see frightening visions and sometimes die of suffocation. Envy was believed to bring it on.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ammuntadore-c01",
        "ammuntadore-c04",
        "ammuntadore-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebagian sarjana menganggap kepercayaan ini muncul setelah Roma merebut Sardinia pada 241 SM, dan ammuntadore sangat mirip incubus Romawi. Lama-kelamaan ia kehilangan sifat roh malam dan dikaitkan dengan Setan.",
      "en": "Some scholars date the belief to after Rome took Sardinia in 241 BC, and the ammuntadore closely resembles the Roman incubus. Over time it lost its night-spirit character and became associated with Satan.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ammuntadore-c02",
        "ammuntadore-c03",
        "ammuntadore-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c01",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s1",
      "quote": "L'ammuntadòre (dal sardo ammuntare/-ai \"avere incubi\") è una creatura della mitologia sarda che attaccherebbe le persone nel sonno tramite gli incubi (in sardo chiamati ammuntadùras).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ammuntadòre (dari bahasa Sardinia ammuntare, \"bermimpi buruk\") adalah makhluk mitologi Sardinia yang menyerang orang dalam tidur lewat mimpi buruk (ammuntadùras).",
        "en": "The ammuntadòre (from Sardinian ammuntare, 'to have nightmares') is a creature of Sardinian mythology that attacks people in their sleep through nightmares (ammuntadùras)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c02",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s1",
      "quote": "Per alcuni studiosi tale credenza ebbe principalmente origine dopo il 241 a.C., ovvero dopo la cattura dell'isola da parte dei romani a seguito della vittoria riportata sui Cartaginesi, ma per altri la Sardegna avrebbe sviluppato queste superstizioni già molto prima.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian sarjana menganggap kepercayaan ini muncul setelah 241 SM, saat Roma merebut pulau itu dari Kartago, sementara yang lain menganggapnya jauh lebih tua.",
        "en": "Some scholars think the belief arose after 241 BC, when Rome took the island from Carthage, while others think Sardinia had such superstitions much earlier."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c03",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s1",
      "quote": "L'Ammuntadore avrebbe perso col tempo il suo carattere di spirito notturno assumendone uno più \"cristiano\" comunemente associato a quello di Satana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lama-kelamaan Ammuntadore kehilangan sifatnya sebagai roh malam dan mendapat watak yang lebih \"Kristen\", biasanya dikaitkan dengan Setan.",
        "en": "Over time the Ammuntadore lost its character as a night spirit and took on a more 'Christian' one, usually associated with Satan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c04",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s1",
      "quote": "Tali sintomi coinciderebbero con forte pressione sul petto, visioni macabre e spaventose in alcuni casi la morte della vittima per soffocamento.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gejala korbannya adalah tekanan kuat di dada, penglihatan mengerikan, dan kadang kematian karena sesak napas.",
        "en": "Its victims' symptoms are strong pressure on the chest, macabre and frightening visions and sometimes death by suffocation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c05",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s1",
      "quote": "Da queste descrizioni l'ammuntadore e gli incubi sono molto simili e sono quindi probabilmente lo stesso demone o ente con un diverso nome.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dari gambaran ini, ammuntadore dan incubus Romawi sangat mirip dan mungkin makhluk yang sama dengan nama berbeda.",
        "en": "From these descriptions the ammuntadore and the Roman incubus are very similar and probably the same demon under another name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ammuntadore-c06",
      "source_id": "ammuntadore-s2",
      "quote": "Si credìat chi fit s'imbìdia a cajonare s'arribu de s'Ammuntadore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Sardinia, orang percaya rasa iri orang lainlah yang mendatangkan Ammuntadore.",
        "en": "According to the Sardinian article, envy was believed to bring on the Ammuntadore."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Sibilla Appenninica adalah penyihir dan peramal dalam legenda pegunungan Norcia, ratu dunia bawah tanah yang dimasuki lewat gua Monte Sibilla.",
    "en": "The Apennine Sibyl is a sorceress and seer of the legends of the Norcia mountains, queen of an underground paradise entered through the cave of Monte Sibilla.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "apennine-sibyl-c01",
      "apennine-sibyl-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Sibilla Appenninica, juga disebut Sibilla dari Norcia, adalah tokoh yang dikenal sejak Abad Pertengahan di pegunungan Piceno dan Norcia, yang memberi nama Monti Sibillini. Menurut legenda rakyat ia penyihir dan peramal, ratu dunia bawah tanah yang indah yang dimasuki lewat gua di puncak Monte Sibilla; para dayangnya dianggap peri.",
      "en": "The Apennine Sibyl, also called the Sibyl of Norcia, is a figure known since the Middle Ages in the mountains of Picenum and Norcia, which gave the Monti Sibillini their name. In popular legend she is a sorceress and seer, queen of a paradisal underworld entered through the cave atop Monte Sibilla; her maidens are thought of as fairies.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "apennine-sibyl-c01",
        "apennine-sibyl-c02",
        "apennine-sibyl-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dua teks utama tentangnya adalah La Salade karya Antoine de La Sale (1420) dan Il Guerrin Meschino karya Andrea da Barberino (sekitar 1410). Agama Kristen menafsirkannya sebagai sosok iblis, dan ia kemudian mengilhami Tannhäuser karya Richard Wagner.",
      "en": "The two main texts about her are Antoine de La Sale's La Salade (1420) and Andrea da Barberino's Il Guerrin Meschino (about 1410). Christianity read her as a demonic figure, and she later inspired Richard Wagner's Tannhäuser.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "apennine-sibyl-c03",
        "apennine-sibyl-c04",
        "apennine-sibyl-c06"
      ]
    }
```
