  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c01",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s2",
      "quote": "La Sibilla Appenninica (detta anche Sibilla Picena o Sibilla di Norcia) è una figura dell'immaginario collettivo diffusasi a partire dal Medioevo nell'area montana del Piceno e di Norcia, in particolare appunto sui Monti Sibillini, ai quali questa ha dato il nome.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sibilla Appenninica (juga Sibilla Picena atau Sibilla dari Norcia) adalah tokoh imajinasi kolektif yang tersebar sejak Abad Pertengahan di pegunungan Piceno dan Norcia, terutama di Monti Sibillini yang dinamai menurut dirinya.",
        "en": "The Apennine Sibyl (also Sibyl of Picenum or of Norcia) is a figure of collective imagination that spread from the Middle Ages in the mountains of Picenum and Norcia, especially the Monti Sibillini named after her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c02",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s2",
      "quote": "Secondo la leggenda popolare, la Sibilla è una maga, incantatrice e indovina; regina di un mondo sotterraneo paradisiaco al quale si accede attraverso la grotta che si apre sulla vetta del Monte Sibilla.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda rakyat, Sibilla adalah penyihir, pemikat, dan peramal, ratu dunia bawah tanah yang indah yang dimasuki lewat gua di puncak Monte Sibilla.",
        "en": "In popular legend the Sibyl is a sorceress, enchantress and seer, queen of a paradisal underground world entered through the cave at the summit of Monte Sibilla."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c03",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s2",
      "quote": "Il Paradiso della Regina Sibilla (Le Paradis de la Reine Sibylle in La Salade), Antoine de La Sale, 1420",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu teks utama tentangnya adalah Le Paradis de la Reine Sibylle dalam La Salade karya Antoine de La Sale (1420).",
        "en": "One of the main texts about her is Le Paradis de la Reine Sibylle in Antoine de La Sale's La Salade (1420)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c04",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s2",
      "quote": "Il Guerrin Meschino, Andrea da Barberino, steso intorno al 1410, ma stampato postumo solo nel 1473",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Teks utama lainnya adalah Il Guerrin Meschino karya Andrea da Barberino, ditulis sekitar 1410 dan baru dicetak pada 1473.",
        "en": "The other main text is Andrea da Barberino's Il Guerrin Meschino, written around 1410 and printed only in 1473."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c05",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s2",
      "quote": "Altri aspetti e prerogative della Sibilla e delle sue damigelle, identificate nel folklore come fate, si apprendono dai racconti degli anziani di Montegallo, Montemonaco, Montefortino, Castelsantangelo sul Nera, Norcia",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri-ciri lain Sibilla dan para dayangnya, yang dalam cerita rakyat dianggap peri, diketahui dari kisah para orang tua di Montegallo, Montemonaco, Montefortino, Castelsantangelo sul Nera, dan Norcia.",
        "en": "Other features of the Sibyl and her maidens, identified in folklore as fairies, come from the tales of elders in Montegallo, Montemonaco, Montefortino, Castelsantangelo sul Nera and Norcia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "apennine-sibyl-c06",
      "source_id": "apennine-sibyl-s1",
      "quote": "Con el advenimiento del cristianismo, el origen pagano de la sibila llevó a una interpretación demoníaca de la figura, que se evidencia, por ejemplo, en la novela caballeresca Il Guerrin Meschino, de Andrea da Barberino. Posteriormente inspiró a autores importantes, como Richard Wagner en su Tannhäuser.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dengan datangnya agama Kristen, asal-usul pagan sang sibil membuatnya ditafsirkan sebagai sosok iblis, seperti dalam Il Guerrin Meschino; ia kemudian mengilhami pengarang seperti Richard Wagner dalam Tannhäuser.",
        "en": "With the coming of Christianity, the sibyl's pagan origin led to a demonic reading of her, as in Il Guerrin Meschino; she later inspired authors such as Richard Wagner in his Tannhäuser."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Arganão adalah makhluk ladang dari tradisi lisan Melgaço, Portugal, yang diam-diam mengamati dan menjahili manusia tanpa pernah terlihat.",
    "en": "The arganão is a field being of the oral tradition of Melgaço, Portugal, that secretly watches and pranks people without ever being seen.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "arganao-c01",
      "arganao-c02",
      "arganao-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Arganão adalah tokoh mitis dari tradisi lisan Melgaço di Portugal utara. Ia tinggal di ladang tetapi sangat penasaran pada kehidupan manusia dan menghabiskan hari dengan diam-diam mengamati mereka. Ia hampir tak terlihat, terhibur oleh tingkah manusia, dan sesekali menjahili atau mengagetkan mereka tanpa pernah tertangkap. Kisahnya tidak tercatat dalam dokumen sebelum awal abad ke-20.",
      "en": "The arganão is a mythical figure of the oral tradition of Melgaço in northern Portugal. He lives in the fields but is intensely curious about human life, spending his days secretly watching people. Almost invisible and amused by human ways, he sometimes plays tricks or gives small frights without ever being caught. His stories were not documented before the early 20th century.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "arganao-c01",
        "arganao-c02",
        "arganao-c03",
        "arganao-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "arganao-c01",
      "source_id": "arganao-s1",
      "quote": "The arganão is a mythical figure, originating from the oral tradition of Melgaço, Portugal.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Arganão adalah tokoh mitis dari tradisi lisan Melgaço, Portugal.",
        "en": "The arganão is a mythical figure from the oral tradition of Melgaço, Portugal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "arganao-c02",
      "source_id": "arganao-s1",
      "quote": "According to popular tales, he lives in the fields, though he can't hide his intense curiosity about people's daily lives. It is said that he spends his days secretly observing them, always attentive to their activities.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita rakyat ia tinggal di ladang, tetapi sangat penasaran pada kehidupan manusia dan menghabiskan hari dengan diam-diam mengamati mereka.",
        "en": "According to popular tales he lives in the fields but is intensely curious about people's daily lives, spending his days secretly watching them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "arganao-c03",
      "source_id": "arganao-s2",
      "quote": "Geralmente é caracterizado como um ser muito esquivo, quase invisível, que se diverte com as atitudes dos humanos, ocasionalmente pregando-lhes algumas partidas e pequenos sustos, sem no entanto ser alguma vez apanhado ou visto por estes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia biasanya digambarkan sangat sulit ditemui, hampir tak terlihat, terhibur oleh tingkah manusia, dan sesekali menjahili serta mengagetkan mereka tanpa pernah tertangkap atau terlihat.",
        "en": "He is usually described as very elusive, almost invisible, amused by human behaviour and occasionally playing tricks and giving small frights, without ever being caught or seen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "arganao-c04",
      "source_id": "arganao-s2",
      "quote": "Os arganões fazem parte da tradição oral regional, sendo que não existem documentos que registem as suas histórias antes do início do século XX.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Arganão adalah bagian tradisi lisan setempat, dan tidak ada dokumen yang mencatat kisahnya sebelum awal abad ke-20.",
        "en": "The arganões are part of regional oral tradition, with no documents recording their stories before the early 20th century."
      }
    }
  ],

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Balāha adalah kuda terbang legendaris dalam mitologi dan cerita rakyat Buddha, dengan versi berbahasa Tionghoa dan India. Seorang bodhisattva datang dalam wujud kuda terbang yang dapat berbicara, sering putih, kadang bersayap atau berkepala gagak, untuk menyelamatkan para pedagang yang terdampar di pulau rakshasa pemakan manusia.       "en": "Balāha is a legendary flying horse of Buddhist mythology and folklore, with Chinese and Indian versions.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "balaha-c01",
      "source_id": "balaha-s1",
      "quote": "Balāha est un cheval volant légendaire de la mythologie et du folklore bouddhiques. Son histoire existe en différente versions, notamment en langue chinoise et en langue indienne.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Balāha adalah kuda terbang legendaris dalam mitologi dan cerita rakyat Buddha, yang kisahnya ada dalam berbagai versi, terutama berbahasa Tionghoa dan India.",
        "en": "Balāha is a legendary flying horse of Buddhist mythology and folklore, whose story exists in various versions, notably in Chinese and Indian languages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balaha-c02",
      "source_id": "balaha-s1",
      "quote": "Un bodhisattva vient, sous la forme d'un cheval volant doué de parole et de raison (souvent blanc, parfois ailé ou encore à tête de corneille) délivrer des marchands hindous échoués sur une île, qui ont par erreur fait confiance à des rākshasas, démons anthropophages.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang bodhisattva datang dalam wujud kuda terbang yang dapat berbicara dan berpikir, sering putih, kadang bersayap atau berkepala gagak, untuk menyelamatkan para pedagang yang terdampar di pulau setelah keliru memercayai rakshasa pemakan manusia.",
        "en": "A bodhisattva comes as a flying horse gifted with speech and reason, often white, sometimes winged or crow-headed, to rescue merchants stranded on an island who mistakenly trusted man-eating rākshasas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balaha-c03",
      "source_id": "balaha-s1",
      "quote": "Dans les Jātaka Valāhassa, ce cheval est le futur Bouddha lui-même, dans l'une de ses précédentes incarnations. Une version plus récente attribue cette métamorphose à Avalokiteshvara, et l'île devient la future Ceylan .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Valāhassa Jātaka kuda itu adalah calon Buddha sendiri dalam salah satu kelahiran sebelumnya; versi yang lebih baru mengaitkan perubahan wujud ini dengan Avalokiteshvara, dan pulau itu menjadi Sri Lanka (Ceylon).",
        "en": "In the Valāhassa Jātaka the horse is the future Buddha himself in a previous birth; a later version ascribes the transformation to Avalokiteshvara, and the island becomes the future Ceylon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balaha-c04",
      "source_id": "balaha-s1",
      "quote": "Tous les marchands s'accrochent aux flancs du cheval comme à ceux d'un navire pour s'échapper et traverser la mer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Semua pedagang berpegangan pada sisi kuda itu seperti pada kapal untuk melarikan diri menyeberangi laut.",
        "en": "All the merchants cling to the horse's flanks as if to a ship to escape across the sea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balaha-c05",
      "source_id": "balaha-s2",
      "quote": "ពលាហ គឺជាសេះហោះក្នុងរឿងព្រេងនិទានព្រះពុទ្ធសាសនា និង រឿងព្រេងនិទាន ។",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Khmer juga menyebut Balāha kuda terbang dalam legenda Buddha.",
        "en": "The Khmer article likewise calls Balāha a flying horse of Buddhist legend."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "Bildur aize is a spirit of Basque mythology that enters dreams, causes nightmares and presses on sleepers' chests.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bildur-aize-c01",
      "bildur-aize-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bildur aize atau Beldur aize, \"angin ketakutan\", adalah roh mitologi Basque yang masuk ke mimpi, menimbulkan mimpi buruk, dan kadang menindih dada orang tidur, mirip Inguma. Konon ia juga meramalkan peristiwa masa depan lewat mimpi. Warga Goierri di Gipuzkoa meminta didoakan pastor di gereja Olaberria, Arriaran, dan Mutiloa agar terbebas darinya.",
      "en": "Bildur aize or Beldur aize, 'wind of fear', is a Basque spirit that enters dreams, causes nightmares and sometimes presses on sleepers' chests, like Inguma. It is also said to foretell the future in dreams. People of the Goierri in Gipuzkoa ask the priests of Olaberria, Arriaran and Mutiloa to pray for them to be freed of it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bildur-aize-c01",
        "bildur-aize-c02",
        "bildur-aize-c03",
        "bildur-aize-c04"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bildur-aize-c01",
      "source_id": "bildur-aize-s1",
      "quote": "Bildur-aize ou Beldur-aize (« vent de la peur » en basque) est un génie de la mythologie basque qui fait faire des cauchemars aux gens et qui parfois oppresse la poitrine des gens qui dorment.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bildur-aize atau Beldur-aize (\"angin ketakutan\" dalam bahasa Basque) adalah roh mitologi Basque yang menimbulkan mimpi buruk dan kadang menindih dada orang yang tidur.",
        "en": "Bildur-aize or Beldur-aize ('wind of fear' in Basque) is a spirit of Basque mythology that gives people nightmares and sometimes oppresses sleepers' chests."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bildur-aize-c02",
      "source_id": "bildur-aize-s1",
      "quote": "C'est un démon, ou esprit, qui entre dans les rêves et provoque des cauchemars. On dit que cet être fantastique prédit des événements futurs dans les rêves",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia iblis atau roh yang masuk ke dalam mimpi dan menimbulkan mimpi buruk, dan konon meramalkan peristiwa masa depan lewat mimpi.",
        "en": "It is a demon or spirit that enters dreams and causes nightmares, and is said to foretell future events in dreams."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bildur-aize-c03",
      "source_id": "bildur-aize-s1",
      "quote": "Pour s'en délivrer, les gens de la région de Goiherri au Guipuscoa se rendent dans certaines églises comme celles d'Olaberria, d'Arriaran et de Mutiloa afin de demander aux les prêtres de ces lieux une prière à leur intention.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk terbebas darinya, warga Goierri di Gipuzkoa pergi ke gereja-gereja tertentu seperti di Olaberria, Arriaran, dan Mutiloa untuk meminta didoakan para pastor di sana.",
        "en": "To be rid of it, people of the Goierri in Gipuzkoa go to certain churches, such as those of Olaberria, Arriaran and Mutiloa, to ask the priests there to pray for them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bildur-aize-c04",
      "source_id": "bildur-aize-s2",
      "quote": "és un esperit o geni malèfic de la mitologia basca que causa malsons i de vegades oprimeix el pit dels dorments, semblantment a allò que fa Inguma.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia roh jahat mitologi Basque yang menimbulkan mimpi buruk dan kadang menindih dada orang tidur, mirip ulah Inguma.",
        "en": "It is an evil spirit of Basque mythology that causes nightmares and sometimes oppresses sleepers' chests, much as Inguma does."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Birao adalah roh kutukan dalam mitologi Basque yang menguasai orang atau hewan yang namanya disebut dalam kutukan.",
    "en": "Birao is the spirit of curses in Basque mythology, possessing the person or animal named in a curse.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "birao-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Birao, \"kutukan\", adalah roh mitologi Basque yang menguasai seseorang atau hewan lewat kutukan yang menyebut namanya, dan dipercaya memasukkan roh jahat gaixtoak ke tubuh korban. Orang Basque percaya kutukan yang ditujukan pada nama akan menimpa apa yang dinamainya; kadang kutukan memakai benda, misalnya koin bengkok yang dipersembahkan kepada santo.",
      "en": "Birao, 'curse', is a Basque spirit that possesses a person or animal through a curse naming it, and was believed to put the evil spirits gaixtoak into the victim. Basques believe a curse laid on a name strikes what it names; sometimes it uses an object, such as a bent coin offered to a saint.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "birao-c01",
        "birao-c02",
        "birao-c03",
        "birao-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Sara kutukan ini juga disebut otoitzgaxto, \"doa jahat\", yang membawa penyakit atau celaka, dan hanya orang yang mengutuk yang dapat menyembuhkannya.",
      "en": "In Sara the curse is also called otoitzgaxto, 'evil prayer', bringing illness or misfortune, and only the one who cursed can lift it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "birao-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "birao-c01",
      "source_id": "birao-s1",
      "quote": "Birao est un Jeinu (Génie) de la mythologie basque qui s'approprie une personne ou un animal en vertu d'une imprécation formulée à l'aide du nom de la personne ou de l'animal en question. Birao signifie en basque « malédiction, gros mot, blasphème ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Birao adalah jeinu (roh) mitologi Basque yang menguasai seseorang atau hewan lewat kutukan yang diucapkan dengan menyebut namanya; birao berarti \"kutukan, makian, hujatan\" dalam bahasa Basque.",
        "en": "Birao is a jeinu (spirit) of Basque mythology that takes possession of a person or animal through a curse spoken with its name; birao means 'curse, swearword, blasphemy' in Basque."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "birao-c02",
      "source_id": "birao-s1",
      "quote": "On croyait que le Birao introduisait des esprits maléfiques appelés gaixtoak (mauvais, au pluriel) dans le corps des personnes visées.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Birao dipercaya memasukkan roh-roh jahat yang disebut gaixtoak (\"yang jahat\") ke tubuh orang yang dikutuk.",
        "en": "Birao was believed to put evil spirits called gaixtoak ('the wicked ones') into the bodies of its targets."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "birao-c03",
      "source_id": "birao-s1",
      "quote": "Parfois, pour l'imprécation, on s'en remet a un objet. Ainsi, en offrant une pièce de monnaie tordue à un saint on exprime de façon concrète que l'on souhaite le mal pour quelqu'un.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang kutukan memakai benda: mempersembahkan koin bengkok kepada seorang santo adalah cara nyata mengharapkan celaka bagi seseorang.",
        "en": "Sometimes the curse relies on an object: offering a bent coin to a saint is a concrete way of wishing someone harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "birao-c04",
      "source_id": "birao-s1",
      "quote": "C'est pourquoi il est répandu au Pays basque la croyance que la malédiction (Birao, Birau) jetée sur un nom atteint par conséquent l'objet qu'il désigne.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena itu di Negeri Basque tersebar kepercayaan bahwa kutukan (Birao, Birau) yang ditujukan pada sebuah nama akan menimpa apa yang dinamainya.",
        "en": "Hence the widespread Basque belief that a curse (Birao, Birau) laid on a name strikes the thing it names."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "birao-c05",
      "source_id": "birao-s2",
      "quote": "A Sara (Lapurdi), anomenen birao i otoitzgaxto ('prec, oració dolenta') aquesta mena d'anatema que, en virtut del poder màgic o adur, porta malaltia o desgràcia a la persona o animal anomenat en la maledicció. Només qui els ha maleït els pot guarir.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Sara (Lapurdi) kutukan semacam ini disebut birao dan otoitzgaxto (\"doa jahat\"); berkat kekuatan magis adur, ia membawa penyakit atau celaka bagi yang namanya disebut, dan hanya orang yang mengutuk yang dapat menyembuhkannya.",
        "en": "In Sara (Lapurdi) this kind of anathema is called birao and otoitzgaxto ('evil prayer'); by the magic power of adur it brings illness or misfortune on whoever is named, and only the one who cursed can cure it."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Borda adalah penyihir mengerikan bermata tertutup dari Lembah Po, Italia, yang muncul dalam kabut dan membunuh orang di dekat rawa.",
    "en": "The Borda is a horrible blindfolded witch of Italy's Po Valley who appears in fog and kills people near marshes.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "borda-legendary-creature-c01",
      "borda-legendary-creature-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Borda adalah makhluk legendaris Emilia-Romagna dan Lembah Po, semacam penyihir mengerikan bermata tertutup yang muncul pada malam hari dan hari berkabut, tinggal di rawa, dan membunuh siapa pun yang bertemu dengannya, terutama anak-anak. Ia personifikasi ketakutan akan rawa dan kanal, dipakai untuk menjauhkan anak dari tempat berbahaya; sebuah nina bobo menyebutnya mengikat anak dengan tali lalu membunuhnya.",
      "en": "The Borda is a legendary creature of Emilia-Romagna and the Po Valley, a horrible blindfolded witch who appears at night and on foggy days, lives in marshes and kills anyone who meets her, especially children. She personifies the fear of marshes and canals, used to keep children from danger; a lullaby says she binds children with a rope and kills them.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "borda-legendary-creature-c01",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c02",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c03",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c06",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam dialek Milan dan Bormio, borda berarti kabut, dan bentuk maskulinnya Bordón atau Bordö berarti raksasa pemakan manusia. Sebagian peneliti merunut namanya ke Borvo, dewa Kelt penguasa mata air panas.",
      "en": "In the Milanese and Bormio dialects borda means fog, and the masculine Bordón or Bordö means ogre. Some scholars trace the name to Borvo, the Celtic god of hot springs.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "borda-legendary-creature-c04",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c05",
        "borda-legendary-creature-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c01",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "The Borda is a legendary creature that belongs to the culture of the Emilia-Romagna and other areas of the Po Valley in Italy.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Borda adalah makhluk legendaris dalam budaya Emilia-Romagna dan daerah lain di Lembah Po, Italia.",
        "en": "The Borda is a legendary creature of the culture of Emilia-Romagna and other parts of the Po Valley in Italy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c02",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "It is a sort of witch that appears, blindfolded and horrible, both at night and on foggy days and kills anyone who has the misfortune to meet her.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia semacam penyihir mengerikan bermata tertutup yang muncul pada malam hari dan hari berkabut, dan membunuh siapa pun yang malang bertemu dengannya.",
        "en": "It is a kind of witch, blindfolded and horrible, that appears at night and on foggy days and kills anyone unlucky enough to meet her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c03",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "It is a personification of the fear related to swamps and marshlands, and to ponds and canals, invoked by adults to scare children and keep them away from such potentially dangerous places.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia personifikasi ketakutan akan rawa, kolam, dan kanal, yang dipakai orang dewasa untuk menakuti anak-anak agar menjauhi tempat berbahaya itu.",
        "en": "It personifies the fear of swamps, ponds and canals, invoked by adults to scare children away from such dangerous places."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c04",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "In Milanese, as well as in the dialects Cremasco and Bormiese, the word borda means fog.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam dialek Milan, Crema, dan Bormio, kata borda berarti kabut.",
        "en": "In Milanese, as in the Crema and Bormio dialects, the word borda means fog."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c05",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "Some scholars of local folklore trace the etymology of the term Borda to the root \"bor-\" which can be traced back to Borvo, of Celtic mythology, who presided over thermal and spring waters",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian peneliti cerita rakyat setempat merunut nama Borda ke akar bor- dari Borvo, dewa Kelt penguasa mata air panas.",
        "en": "Some local folklorists trace the name Borda to the root bor-, going back to Borvo of Celtic mythology, who presided over hot springs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c06",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s1",
      "quote": "Lullaby, the Borda binds beautiful children with a rope.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah lagu nina bobo menyebut Borda mengikat anak-anak manis dengan tali lalu membunuh mereka.",
        "en": "A lullaby says the Borda binds beautiful children with a rope and then kills them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c07",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s2",
      "quote": "La Borda è una creatura leggendaria che appartiene alla tradizione della Pianura Padana ed è nota per abitare in zone acquitrinose nelle quali poteva uccidere tutti i passanti, specialmente bambini.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Italia, Borda tinggal di daerah berawa tempat ia dapat membunuh semua orang yang lewat, terutama anak-anak.",
        "en": "According to the Italian article, the Borda lives in marshy areas where she could kill any passer-by, especially children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borda-legendary-creature-c08",
      "source_id": "borda-legendary-creature-s2",
      "quote": "Al maschile prende il nome di Bordón a Parma, Bordö o Bordoeu a Milano (col significato di Orco)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bentuk maskulinnya disebut Bordón di Parma dan Bordö atau Bordoeu di Milan, yang berarti raksasa pemakan manusia (orco).",
        "en": "The masculine form is called Bordón in Parma and Bordö or Bordoeu in Milan, meaning ogre."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en dan it; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bubota adalah hantu berselubung kain putih dari Mallorca dan Valencia yang menghantui kuburan dan menakuti anak-anak nakal.",
    "en": "The bubota is a sheet-shrouded ghost of Mallorca and Valencia that haunts cemeteries and frightens disobedient children.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bubota-c01",
      "bubota-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bubota adalah hantu dalam mitologi Mallorca dan Valencia yang mengganggu anak-anak nakal dan menghantui kuburan Mallorca serta desa-desa di Valencia tengah. Mereka tampak seperti orang tanpa tubuh yang setengah tembus pandang, berselubung kain besar. Biasanya mereka tak terlihat dan diam; saat bergerak untuk menakuti orang, mereka melayang dan meninggalkan jejak yang membuat orang menggigil.",
      "en": "The bubota is a ghost of the mythology of Mallorca and Valencia that harasses disobedient children and haunts Mallorca's cemeteries and the villages of central Valencia. They look like bodiless, semi-transparent people shrouded in large cloths. Usually invisible and still, when they move to frighten someone they float through the air, leaving a chilling trail.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bubota-c01",
        "bubota-c02",
        "bubota-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Valencia mereka punya banyak nama lokal seperti bumbota dan marmota. Mossèn Alcover mencatat dongeng La bubota blanca de Consell tentang petani yang ditipu pemuda yang menyamar sebagai bubota.",
      "en": "In Valencia they have many local names such as bumbota and marmota. Mossèn Alcover recorded the tale La bubota blanca de Consell about a farmer tricked by a youth disguised as a bubota.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bubota-c04",
        "bubota-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bubota-c01",
      "source_id": "bubota-s1",
      "quote": "Según la mitología mallorquina y valenciana, las bubotas (bubotes en catalán), son fantasmas que acosan a los niños y niñas desobedientes; además, suelen frecuentar los cementerios de la isla de Mallorca y los pueblos de las comarcas centrales valencianas asustando a los vivos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitologi Mallorca dan Valencia, bubota (bahasa Katalan: bubotes) adalah hantu yang mengganggu anak-anak nakal dan sering muncul di kuburan Mallorca dan desa-desa di Valencia tengah untuk menakuti orang hidup.",
        "en": "In the mythology of Mallorca and Valencia, bubotas (Catalan bubotes) are ghosts that harass disobedient children and haunt the cemeteries of Mallorca and the villages of central Valencia, frightening the living."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bubota-c02",
      "source_id": "bubota-s1",
      "quote": "Tienen la apariencia de personas incorpóreas y semitransparentes cubiertas totalmente por grandes telas que les tapan la cabeza y los brazos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka tampak seperti orang tanpa tubuh yang setengah tembus pandang, seluruhnya tertutup kain besar yang menutupi kepala dan lengan.",
        "en": "They look like bodiless, semi-transparent people entirely covered by large cloths over their heads and arms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bubota-c03",
      "source_id": "bubota-s1",
      "quote": "Normalmente permanecen invisibles, paradas en puntos concretos. Cuando se mueven, lo hacen para asustar a alguien y lo hacen levitando por el aire, dejando un rastro que produce escalofríos mortales a los seres vivos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biasanya mereka tak terlihat dan diam di tempat tertentu; saat bergerak untuk menakuti orang, mereka melayang dan meninggalkan jejak yang membuat makhluk hidup menggigil.",
        "en": "They usually remain invisible, standing still at certain spots; when they move to scare someone, they float through the air, leaving a trail that gives the living deadly chills."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bubota-c04",
      "source_id": "bubota-s2",
      "quote": "Al País Valencià, aquests éssers reben un gran nombre de denominacions locals a més de bubota: bumbota, bumberota, mumorota, marmota, muserota, mumaranta, etc.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Valencia makhluk ini punya banyak nama lokal selain bubota, seperti bumbota, bumberota, mumorota, marmota, dan muserota.",
        "en": "In Valencia these beings have many local names besides bubota, such as bumbota, bumberota, mumorota, marmota and muserota."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bubota-c05",
      "source_id": "bubota-s2",
      "quote": "Mossèn Alcover recollí rondalles a les quals apareixen bubotes, com ara La bubota blanca de Consell, en la qual un llaurador poruc i ingenu és enganat per tres joves un dels quals es disfressa de bubota amb una camisa blanca de dona",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mossèn Alcover mencatat dongeng tentang bubota, misalnya La bubota blanca de Consell, tentang petani penakut yang ditipu tiga pemuda, salah satunya menyamar sebagai bubota dengan kemeja putih perempuan.",
        "en": "Mossèn Alcover collected tales featuring bubotes, such as La bubota blanca de Consell, in which a timid farmer is tricked by three youths, one disguised as a bubota in a woman's white shirt."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Carcamán adalah ras raksasa mitologis dari pulau Arousa, Galisia, yang namanya kini menjadi sebutan bagi penduduk pulau itu.",
    "en": "The carcamáns are a mythological race of giants of the island of Arousa, Galicia, whose name now serves as a nickname for the islanders.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "carcaman-c01",
      "carcaman-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Carcamán adalah ras raksasa mitologis yang tinggal di pulau Arousa. Menurut berbagai versi mereka terkait castro di utara pulau, atau pedagang, atau bajak laut. Kini istilah ini menjadi sebutan populer bagi penduduk pulau, dan juga berarti kapal besar yang berat atau lelaki malas.",
      "en": "The carcamáns are a mythological race of giants who lived on the island of Arousa. In various versions they were tied to the hillfort in the north of the island, or were merchants, or pirates. Today the term is a popular nickname for the islanders and also means a big heavy boat or a lazy man.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "carcaman-c01",
        "carcaman-c02",
        "carcaman-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal istilahnya tidak pasti: mungkin pedagang Italia yang singgah di Galisia pada abad ke-17 dan ke-18, atau penyelundup Arousa pada masa perang.",
      "en": "The origin of the term is uncertain: perhaps Italian merchants calling in Galicia in the 17th and 18th centuries, or the smugglers of Arousa in wartime.",

        "carcaman-c04",
        "carcaman-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "publisher": "Wikipedia (gl)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "gl",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "carcaman-c01",
      "source_id": "carcaman-s2",
      "quote": "Os carcamáns son unha raza mitolóxica de xigantes que vivían na Illa de Arousa. Segundo as diversas versións, estaban ligados ao castro do norte da illa, eran comerciantes ou estaban dedicados á pirataría.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Carcamán adalah ras raksasa mitologis yang tinggal di pulau Arousa; menurut berbagai versi mereka terkait castro di utara pulau, atau pedagang, atau bajak laut.",
        "en": "The carcamáns are a mythological race of giants who lived on the island of Arousa; in various versions they were linked to the hillfort in the north of the island, or were merchants, or pirates."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carcaman-c02",
      "source_id": "carcaman-s2",
      "quote": "Hoxe, o termo pervive como xentilicio popular para designar ós veciños da Illa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kini istilah itu bertahan sebagai sebutan populer bagi penduduk pulau tersebut.",
        "en": "Today the term survives as a popular demonym for the island's inhabitants."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carcaman-c03",
      "source_id": "carcaman-s2",
      "quote": "Baixo o termo de carcamán tamén se designa un barco grande e pesado e, por extensión, un home lacazán que non quere facer esforzos ou ben un home vello e achacoso.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata carcamán juga berarti kapal besar yang berat, dan secara kiasan lelaki malas atau lelaki tua yang sakit-sakitan.",
        "en": "Carcamán also means a big, heavy boat and, by extension, a lazy man or a frail old man."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carcaman-c04",
      "source_id": "carcaman-s1",
      "quote": "Alguns historiadores acreditam que o termo se referia originalmente aos comerciantes italianos que navegavam da Península Itálica até à Flandres, parando na Galiza durante os séculos XVII e XVIII.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian sejarawan percaya istilah ini awalnya merujuk pedagang Italia yang berlayar dari Italia ke Flandria dan singgah di Galisia pada abad ke-17 dan ke-18.",
        "en": "Some historians believe the term originally meant Italian merchants sailing from Italy to Flanders and stopping in Galicia in the 17th and 18th centuries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carcaman-c05",
      "source_id": "carcaman-s1",
      "quote": "Outra hipótese sugere que o termo foi usado para descrever os contrabandistas da Arousa, especialmente durante os períodos de guerra e pós-guerra",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hipotesis lain menyebut istilah ini dipakai untuk penyelundup Arousa, terutama pada masa perang dan sesudahnya.",
        "en": "Another hypothesis holds the term described the smugglers of Arousa, especially in wartime and after."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Asal istilah carcamán",
        "en": "Origin of the term carcamán"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Pedagang Italia.",
            "en": "Italian merchants."

        {
          "summary": {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Carmençó adalah roh berbulu hitam dalam mitologi Katalan yang menjaga pondok gembala di musim dingin, tetapi melihatnya membawa kemalangan.",
    "en": "The carmençó is a black, woolly spirit of Catalan mythology that keeps shepherds' huts standing in winter, though seeing it brings misfortune.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "carmenco-c01",
      "carmenco-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Carmençó adalah roh mitologi Katalan yang berkeliaran di padang gunung, hitam seperti jelaga dan berbulu dari kepala sampai kaki. Ia menempati pondok gembala saat kosong di musim dingin dan menjaganya agar tidak roboh. Para gembala tua Empordà takut sekaligus menghormatinya, karena melihatnya membawa bertahun-tahun kemalangan, sehingga mereka melempar tujuh batu dengan ketapel untuk mengusirnya saat kembali ke pondok.",
      "en": "The carmençó is a spirit of Catalan mythology that roams mountain meadows, black as soot and woolly all over. It occupies shepherds' huts when they stand empty in winter and keeps them from collapsing. The old shepherds of the Empordà both fear and respect it, since seeing it brings years of misfortune, and so sling seven stones to drive it out when they return to the hut.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "carmenco-c01",
        "carmenco-c02",
        "carmenco-c03"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "carmenco-c01",
      "source_id": "carmenco-s1",
      "quote": "El carmençó és un geni de la mitologia catalana, que ronda pels prats de muntanya, negre com el sutge i llanut de cap a peus. S'instal·la a les cabanes dels pastors quan aquests no les fan servir, principalment a l'hivern, evitant que s'esfondrin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Carmençó adalah roh mitologi Katalan yang berkeliaran di padang gunung, hitam seperti jelaga dan berbulu dari kepala sampai kaki; ia menempati pondok gembala saat tidak dipakai, terutama di musim dingin, dan menjaganya agar tidak roboh.",
        "en": "The carmençó is a spirit of Catalan mythology that roams mountain meadows, black as soot and woolly from head to toe; it settles in shepherds' huts when they are unused, mainly in winter, keeping them from collapsing."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carmenco-c02",
      "source_id": "carmenco-s1",
      "quote": "És un ésser temut i respectat alhora pels vells pastors empordanesos, perquè veure'l comporta molts anys de desgràcia. És per aquest motiu que, quan pugen de terra baixa, llencen set pedrades amb la fona per foragitar-lo de la cabana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia ditakuti sekaligus dihormati para gembala tua Empordà, karena melihatnya membawa bertahun-tahun kemalangan; karena itu saat naik dari dataran rendah mereka melempar tujuh batu dengan ketapel untuk mengusirnya dari pondok.",
        "en": "It is both feared and respected by the old shepherds of the Empordà, since seeing it brings many years of misfortune; so when they come up from the lowlands they sling seven stones to drive it from the hut."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carmenco-c03",
      "source_id": "carmenco-s2",
      "quote": "Empordàko artzain zaharrek aldi berean beldurtzen eta errespetatzen duten izaki bat da, urte askotako zorigaitza baitakar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Basque juga mencatat bahwa ia ditakuti dan dihormati para gembala tua Empordà karena membawa bertahun-tahun kemalangan.",
        "en": "The Basque article also notes it is feared and respected by the old shepherds of the Empordà because it brings years of misfortune."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cegua adalah hantu legenda Amerika Tengah yang muncul sebagai perempuan cantik di jalan sepi lalu berubah menjadi monster berkepala kuda.",
    "en": "The Cegua is a ghost of Central American legend that appears as a beautiful woman on lonely roads and turns into a horse-headed monster.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cegua-c01",
      "cegua-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cegua, dari kata Nahuatl cihuatl, \"perempuan\", adalah hantu dalam legenda Meksiko dan Amerika Tengah. Ia muncul malam hari di jalan sepi dan meminta diantar ke desa. Kepada lelaki hidung belang ia tampak sebagai perempuan sangat cantik, lalu setelah naik ke kuda atau kendaraan berubah menjadi monster berkepala tengkorak kuda yang membusuk.",
      "en": "The Cegua, from Nahuatl cihuatl, 'woman', is a ghost of Mexican and Central American legend. She appears at night on lonely roads asking to be taken to a village. To womanisers she looks like a very beautiful woman, then, once on the horse or vehicle, turns into a monster with a rotting horse skull for a head.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cegua-c01",
        "cegua-c02",
        "cegua-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mitosnya berasal dari Mesoamerika, berkaitan dengan Siguanaba dan La Llorona, tetapi wajah kudanya lahir dari sinkretisme setelah kedatangan orang Spanyol. Sebutan cegua terutama dipakai di Nikaragua dan Kosta Rika.",
      "en": "Her myth is Mesoamerican, related to the Siguanaba and La Llorona, but her horse face arose from syncretism after the Spaniards came. The name cegua is used mainly in Nicaragua and Costa Rica.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cegua-c03",
        "cegua-c04",
        "cegua-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "title": "Cegua",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (no)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "no",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cegua-c01",
      "source_id": "cegua-s1",
      "quote": "La Cegua (del náhuatl, cihuatl, mujer),​​ también conocida como Ceguanaba,​ Segua o Tzegua, es un personaje de las leyendas mexicanas y centroamericanas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cegua (dari bahasa Nahuatl cihuatl, \"perempuan\"), dikenal juga sebagai Ceguanaba, Segua, atau Tzegua, adalah tokoh legenda Meksiko dan Amerika Tengah.",
        "en": "The Cegua (from Nahuatl cihuatl, 'woman'), also known as Ceguanaba, Segua or Tzegua, is a figure of Mexican and Central American legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cegua-c02",
      "source_id": "cegua-s1",
      "quote": "Es un ser espectral que se materializa por las noches en caminos solitarios, solicitando ayuda para que la lleven a algún poblado cercano. El espanto generalmente se aparece a los hombres mujeriegos en la forma de una mujer muy hermosa, la cual luego se transforma en un monstruo con la cabeza de un caballo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia makhluk hantu yang muncul malam hari di jalan sepi, meminta diantar ke desa terdekat; biasanya ia menampakkan diri kepada lelaki hidung belang sebagai perempuan sangat cantik yang lalu berubah menjadi monster berkepala kuda.",
        "en": "It is a spectral being that appears at night on lonely roads asking to be taken to a nearby village; it usually appears to womanisers as a very beautiful woman who then turns into a monster with a horse's head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cegua-c03",
      "source_id": "cegua-s1",
      "quote": "El mito de la Cegua es de origen mesoamericano y está relacionado con el mito de la Siguanaba, con algunas variantes, y en parte, con el de la Llorona.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitos Cegua berasal dari Mesoamerika dan berkaitan dengan mitos Siguanaba serta sebagian dengan La Llorona.",
        "en": "The Cegua myth is Mesoamerican in origin and related to the Siguanaba myth and partly to La Llorona."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cegua-c04",
      "source_id": "cegua-s1",
      "quote": "Al igual que con la leyenda del Cadejo, entonces, la Cegua proviene del sincretismo cultural que se insertó con la llegada de los españoles a América, ya que el concepto del rostro de caballo aún no existía en los tiempos prehispánicos",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti legenda Cadejo, Cegua lahir dari sinkretisme budaya setelah kedatangan orang Spanyol, karena wajah kuda belum dikenal pada masa pra-Hispanik.",
        "en": "Like the Cadejo legend, the Cegua arose from cultural syncretism after the Spaniards arrived, since the horse face did not exist in pre-Hispanic times."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cegua-c05",
      "source_id": "cegua-s1",
      "quote": "El término cegua para designar a este ser espectral es de uso predominante en Nicaragua y Costa Rica",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebutan cegua terutama dipakai di Nikaragua dan Kosta Rika.",
        "en": "The term cegua is used mainly in Nicaragua and Costa Rica."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cegua-c06",
      "source_id": "cegua-s2",
      "quote": "Når hun har satt seg opp på hesten, eller kjøretøyet i moderne versjoner av legenden, forvandler hun seg til en fryktelig skapning med en råtnende hestehodeskalle som hode.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Norwegia, setelah naik ke kuda, atau kendaraan dalam versi modern, ia berubah menjadi makhluk mengerikan berkepala tengkorak kuda yang membusuk.",
        "en": "According to the Norwegian article, once she is up on the horse, or vehicle in modern versions, she turns into a dreadful creature with a rotting horse skull for a head."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Coulobre adalah naga bersayap yang menghantui mata air Fontaine-de-Vaucluse sampai diusir Santo Véran, uskup Cavaillon.",
    "en": "The Coulobre is a winged dragon that haunted the spring of Fontaine-de-Vaucluse until driven out by Saint Véran, bishop of Cavaillon.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "coulobre-c01",
      "coulobre-c02",
      "coulobre-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Coulobre adalah semacam drac atau naga bersayap yang menghantui mata air Sorgue di Fontaine-de-Vaucluse. Makhluk betina yang hidup sendiri ini konon ditinggalkan naga-naga pasangannya dan harus membesarkan sendiri anak-anaknya, salamander hitam berbintik emas. Santo Véran, uskup Cavaillon, mengusirnya, dan ia terbang ke Alpen lalu mati di Saint-Véran, tempat patungnya ada di serambi gereja.",
      "en": "The Coulobre is a kind of drac or winged dragon that haunted the spring of the Sorgue at Fontaine-de-Vaucluse. This solitary female was said to be abandoned by her dragon mates and left to raise alone her black, gold-spotted salamander young. Saint Véran, bishop of Cavaillon, drove her off, and she flew to the Alps to die at Saint-Véran, where she is carved on the church porch.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "coulobre-c01",
        "coulobre-c02",
        "coulobre-c03",
        "coulobre-c04",
        "coulobre-c07"
      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c01",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "Le Coulobre est une créature fantastique, sorte de drac ou dragon, qui hantait la fontaine de Vaucluse.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Coulobre adalah makhluk khayalan, semacam drac atau naga, yang menghantui mata air Fontaine-de-Vaucluse.",
        "en": "The Coulobre is a fantastic creature, a kind of drac or dragon, that haunted the spring of Fontaine-de-Vaucluse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c02",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "Selon la légende, saint Véran, évêque de Cavaillon, en aurait miraculeusement débarrassé la rivière Sorgue.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, Santo Véran, uskup Cavaillon, secara ajaib membebaskan sungai Sorgue darinya.",
        "en": "According to legend, Saint Véran, bishop of Cavaillon, miraculously rid the river Sorgue of it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c03",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "était une créature ailée qui vivait dans l'exsurgence de la Sorgue. Elle passait pour s'unir avec des dragons qui l'abandonnaient ensuite, le forçant à élever seul les petites salamandres noires tachetées d'or dont elle accouchait.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia makhluk bersayap yang hidup di mata air Sorgue; konon ia kawin dengan naga-naga yang lalu meninggalkannya, sehingga ia harus membesarkan sendiri anak-anaknya, salamander hitam berbintik emas.",
        "en": "It was a winged creature living in the spring of the Sorgue; it supposedly mated with dragons who then abandoned it, leaving it to raise alone the little black, gold-spotted salamanders it bore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c04",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "Selon la légende, Véran chassa « cette bête immonde » qui s'envola vers les Alpes où elle s'en fut mourir. Le village de Saint-Véran aurait été son lieu de chute. Le monstre se trouve d'ailleurs statufié sous le porche de l'église.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Véran mengusir \"binatang najis\" itu, yang terbang ke Pegunungan Alpen dan mati di sana, konon di desa Saint-Véran, tempat patungnya ada di bawah serambi gereja.",
        "en": "Véran drove off 'this foul beast', which flew to the Alps to die, supposedly at the village of Saint-Véran, where it is carved under the church porch."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c05",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "Pétrarque aurait été attaqué par cette créature alors qu'il se trouvait au bord de l'eau avec sa bien-aimée : il tua le monstre d'un coup d'épée mais Laure mourut ensuite de la peste.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kebangkitan mitos abad ke-15, Petrarca konon diserang makhluk ini di tepi air bersama kekasihnya dan membunuhnya dengan pedang, tetapi Laura kemudian mati karena wabah.",
        "en": "In the 15th-century revival of the myth, Petrarch was supposedly attacked by the creature by the water with his beloved and killed it with his sword, but Laura later died of the plague."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c06",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s1",
      "quote": "Le Coulobre est évoqué dans le jeu vidéo Dordogne, sorti en 2023.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Coulobre disebut dalam permainan video Dordogne yang dirilis pada 2023.",
        "en": "The Coulobre is mentioned in the video game Dordogne, released in 2023."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coulobre-c07",
      "source_id": "coulobre-s2",
      "quote": "По легенде Кулобр — существо женского рода. Она живёт в одиночестве и пытается найти себе пару.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, Coulobre adalah makhluk betina yang hidup sendirian dan mencari pasangan.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, the Coulobre is female, living alone and seeking a mate."
      }
    }
  ],

Menurut Theogonia ia membawa hujan ke benua Eropa, dan namanya mungkin menjadi asal nama benua itu, meski hal ini diperdebatkan.",

      "id": "europa-q3060724-c01",
      "source_id": "europa-q3060724-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la mythologie grecque archaïque, Europe (en grec ancien Εὐρώπη / Eurôpê) est une Océanide et une Néphélée, fille d'Océan et de Téthys citée par Hésiode dans sa liste d'Océanides.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Yunani arkais, Europa (Εὐρώπη) adalah Okeanid sekaligus Nephele (nimfa awan), putri Okeanos dan Tethys yang disebut Hesiodos dalam daftar Okeanidnya.",
        "en": "In archaic Greek mythology Europa (Εὐρώπη) is an Oceanid and a Nephele (cloud nymph), daughter of Oceanus and Tethys, named by Hesiod in his list of Oceanids."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "europa-q3060724-c02",
      "source_id": "europa-q3060724-s1",
      "quote": "Selon la Théogonie d'Hésiode, elle apporte la pluie sur le continent européen, dont elle pourrait être à l'origine du nom (ce point est débattu).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Theogonia karya Hesiodos ia membawa hujan ke benua Eropa, dan namanya mungkin menjadi asal nama benua itu, meski hal ini diperdebatkan.",
        "en": "According to Hesiod's Theogony she brings rain to the European continent, whose name she may have inspired, though this is debated."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "europa-q3060724-c03",
      "source_id": "europa-q3060724-s1",
      "quote": "Elle est l'une de leur multiples filles, les Océanides, généralement au nombre de trois mille, et a pour frères les Dieux-fleuve, eux aussi au nombre de trois mille.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia salah satu dari putri-putri Okeanos dan Tethys yang biasanya berjumlah tiga ribu, dengan tiga ribu saudara laki-laki para dewa sungai.",
        "en": "She is one of the many daughters of Oceanus and Tethys, usually three thousand, with three thousand brothers, the river gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "europa-q3060724-c04",
      "source_id": "europa-q3060724-s2",
      "quote": "Hervez Theogonia Hesiodos eo ganti e vez degaset glav war zouar Europa, ar c'hevandir.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Breton juga menyebut bahwa menurut Theogonia Hesiodos ia membawa hujan ke benua Eropa.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Facanito adalah setan kecil yang makan baja tumbuk dalam cerita rakyat Trás-os-Montes, kini juga sebutan anak bertopeng careto dalam karnaval.",
    "en": "The facanito is a little devil that eats ground steel in the folklore of Trás-os-Montes, now also the name of a child masked as a careto at carnival.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "facanito-c01",
      "facanito-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pada Abad Pertengahan di Trás-os-Montes, facanito disebut \"setan kecil yang makan baja tumbuk\", dan di Miranda do Douro namanya juga dipakai untuk makhluk mitologis yang sangat kecil dan gelisah. Kini facanito adalah anak yang memakai topeng careto dan meniru para careto dalam karnaval sebagai ritual inisiasinya sendiri; tradisi careto diduga berakar pada budaya Kelt pra-Romawi.",
      "en": "In medieval Trás-os-Montes the facanito was called 'a little devil that feeds on ground steel', and in Miranda do Douro the name also refers to a very small, restless mythological being. Today the facanito is a child who wears a careto mask and imitates the caretos at carnival as his own initiation rite; the careto tradition is thought to have Celtic, pre-Roman roots.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "facanito-c01",
        "facanito-c02",
        "facanito-c03",
        "facanito-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "facanito-c01",
      "source_id": "facanito-s1",
      "quote": "O facanito é uma criança que se mascara de “careto” e tenta imitar os caretos nas suas folias, cumprindo o seu próprio ritual de iniciação e garantindo a continuidade da tradição.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Facanito adalah anak yang memakai topeng careto dan meniru para careto dalam pesta mereka, menjalankan ritual inisiasinya sendiri dan menjaga kelangsungan tradisi.",
        "en": "The facanito is a child who masks as a careto and imitates the caretos in their revels, performing his own initiation rite and keeping the tradition alive."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "facanito-c02",
      "source_id": "facanito-s1",
      "quote": "Na Idade Média em Trás-os-Montes era referido como um \"diabinho que se alimenta de aço moído\". Em Miranda do Douro é o nome que se dá a um homem de pequena estatura com jeito de provocador e é também como se designa um ser mitológico, muito pequeno e irrequieto.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Abad Pertengahan di Trás-os-Montes ia disebut \"setan kecil yang makan baja tumbuk\"; di Miranda do Douro namanya dipakai untuk lelaki bertubuh kecil yang suka memancing keributan, dan juga untuk makhluk mitologis yang sangat kecil dan gelisah.",
        "en": "In the Middle Ages in Trás-os-Montes it was called 'a little devil that feeds on ground steel'; in Miranda do Douro the name is given to a small, provocative man, and also to a mythological being, very small and restless."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "facanito-c03",
      "source_id": "facanito-s2",
      "quote": "L careto ye ua figura maçcarada de l Antruido de  Trás ls Montes i Alto Douro, an Pertual.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Careto adalah tokoh bertopeng dalam karnaval (Entrudo) Trás-os-Montes dan Alto Douro di Portugal.",
        "en": "The careto is a masked figure of the carnival (Entrudo) of Trás-os-Montes and Alto Douro in Portugal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "facanito-c04",
      "source_id": "facanito-s2",
      "quote": "Pensa-se que la tradiçon de ls caretos tenga raízes célticas, de la era pré-romana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tradisi careto diduga berakar pada budaya Kelt pra-Romawi.",
        "en": "The careto tradition is thought to have Celtic, pre-Roman roots."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Farfadet adalah peri kecil keriput dari cerita rakyat Prancis, terutama Vendée dan Poitou, yang membantu di pertanian dengan imbalan susu.",
    "en": "The farfadet is a small, wrinkled sprite of French folklore, especially of the Vendée and Poitou, who helps on farms in return for milk.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "farfadet-c01",
      "farfadet-c02",
      "farfadet-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Farfadet adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Prancis, mirip brownie atau pixie, yang khas Vendée dan Poitou dan dikenal sebagai fadet di Provence. Mereka kecil, sekitar setengah meter, keriput, berkulit cokelat, dan berpakaian compang-camping atau telanjang. Namanya dari bahasa Provençal farfadet, bentuk penguat dari fadet, \"peri\".",
      "en": "The farfadet is a creature of French folklore, akin to the brownie or pixie, belonging especially to the Vendée and Poitou and known in Provence as the fadet. Farfadets are small, about half a metre tall, wrinkled, brown-skinned, and wear rags or nothing. The name comes from Provençal farfadet, an intensified form of fadet, 'fairy'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "farfadet-c01",
        "farfadet-c02",
        "farfadet-c03",
        "farfadet-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka lebih suka menolong daripada jahat: merawat kuda, mengepang surai, dan mengerjakan tugas ladang dengan imbalan semangkuk susu atau krim, tetapi pergi bila diberi pakaian baru. A. V. C. Berbiguier yang merasa disiksa mereka menulis otobiografi tentangnya pada 1821.",
      "en": "They are helpful rather than malicious: tending horses, braiding manes and doing field work for a bowl of milk or cream, but leaving if given new clothes. A. V. C. Berbiguier, who felt tormented by them, wrote an autobiography about it in 1821.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "farfadet-c04",
        "farfadet-c05",
        "farfadet-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c01",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "Farfadets are creatures of French folklore. The word translates variously as \"Sprite\", \"Imp\",  \"Brownie\", or \"Leprechaun\", though they also resemble the Pixies of Britain's West Country.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Farfadet adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Prancis; katanya dapat diterjemahkan sebagai sprite, imp, brownie, atau leprechaun, dan mereka juga mirip pixie dari West Country Britania.",
        "en": "Farfadets are creatures of French folklore; the word is rendered as sprite, imp, brownie or leprechaun, and they also resemble the pixies of Britain's West Country."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c02",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "The term is in general use throughout France, though the creature is local particularly to the Vendée and Poitou regions. Farfadets also  feature in Occitan mythology, particularly in Provence, where they are known as fadets.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah ini dipakai di seluruh Prancis, tetapi makhluknya khas Vendée dan Poitou; farfadet juga ada dalam mitologi Occitania, terutama di Provence, dengan nama fadet.",
        "en": "The term is used throughout France, though the creature belongs especially to the Vendée and Poitou; farfadets also appear in Occitan mythology, especially in Provence, as fadets."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c03",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "Farfadets are described as being small (some half-meter tall), wrinkled, and brown-skinned; they generally wear tattered brown clothing, or go naked.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka kecil (sekitar setengah meter), keriput, dan berkulit cokelat, biasanya berpakaian cokelat compang-camping atau telanjang.",
        "en": "They are small (about half a metre tall), wrinkled and brown-skinned, usually in tattered brown clothes or naked."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c04",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "They like to tend horses, which they will groom, or weave ringlets into their manes. They live in woodland, but will also attach themselves to a neighbouring farm or homestead, and complete odd tasks in the fields, in return for a bowl of milk or cream left on the doorstep at night.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka suka merawat kuda, menyisir atau mengepang surainya; mereka tinggal di hutan tetapi juga menempel pada pertanian terdekat dan mengerjakan tugas di ladang dengan imbalan semangkuk susu atau krim di depan pintu pada malam hari.",
        "en": "They like to tend horses, grooming them or braiding their manes; they live in woods but also attach themselves to a nearby farm, doing odd jobs in the fields for a bowl of milk or cream left on the doorstep at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c05",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "However they can be frightened away by too much kindness, such as leaving new clothes out for them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun kebaikan yang berlebihan, seperti meninggalkan pakaian baru untuk mereka, dapat membuat mereka pergi.",
        "en": "Yet too much kindness, such as leaving new clothes for them, can frighten them away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c06",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s1",
      "quote": "In 1821 he was constrained to publish an autobiography, Les Farfadets ou Tous les démons ne sont pas de l'autre monde",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penulis Prancis A. V. C. Berbiguier yang merasa disiksa farfadet menerbitkan otobiografi Les Farfadets ou Tous les démons ne sont pas de l'autre monde pada 1821.",
        "en": "The French writer A. V. C. Berbiguier, who believed farfadets tormented him, published the autobiography Les Farfadets ou Tous les démons ne sont pas de l'autre monde in 1821."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "farfadet-c07",
      "source_id": "farfadet-s2",
      "quote": "Farfadet est un mot emprunté au provençal farfadet (« lutin ») du XVIe siècle, probablement forme renforcée de fadet, dérivé lui-même de fada, fado signifiant « fée ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata farfadet dipinjam dari bahasa Provençal farfadet (\"lutin\") abad ke-16, mungkin bentuk penguat dari fadet, yang berasal dari fada atau fado, \"peri\".",
        "en": "Farfadet is borrowed from 16th-century Provençal farfadet ('sprite'), probably an intensified form of fadet, itself from fada or fado, 'fairy'."
      }
    }
  ],

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Banteng Hutan adalah banteng buas pemakan daging dari Etiopia dalam bestiari kuno, berkulit kebal senjata dan bertanduk yang dapat berputar.",
    "en": "The Forest Bull is a fierce, flesh-eating bull of Ethiopia in ancient bestiaries, with weapon-proof hide and swivelling horns.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "forest-bull-c01",
      "forest-bull-c02",
      "forest-bull-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Banteng Hutan atau Banteng Etiopia adalah hewan bestiari kuno dan abad pertengahan. Menurut Plinius Tua mereka sapi buas berwarna cokelat kekuningan di Etiopia, bermulut menganga sampai telinga, dengan tanduk yang dapat berputar ke segala arah. Aelianus menyebut mereka \"pemakan daging\", dua kali lebih besar dari banteng biasa, sangat cepat, dan berkulit begitu keras sehingga senjata besi memantul.",
      "en": "The Forest Bull or Ethiopian Bull is an animal of ancient and medieval bestiaries. According to Pliny the Elder they were fierce tawny cattle of Ethiopia with mouths gaping to the ears and horns that could swivel any way. Aelian calls them 'flesh eaters', twice the size of ordinary bulls, very fast, with hide so tough that iron glanced off it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "forest-bull-c01",
        "forest-bull-c02",
        "forest-bull-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Para gembala melindungi ternak dengan menggali parit dalam tempat banteng itu jatuh dan tercekik oleh amarahnya. Hewan ini mungkin berdasarkan catatan tentang kerbau Afrika.",
      "en": "Herdsmen protected their flocks by digging deep ditches where the bulls fell and choked on their rage. The animal may be based on accounts of the African buffalo.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "forest-bull-c04",
        "forest-bull-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {

      "url": "https://nl.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tauros_Aithiopikos",
      "title": "Tauros Aithiopikos",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (nl)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "nl",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "forest-bull-c01",
      "source_id": "forest-bull-s1",
      "quote": "is an animal from ancient and medieval bestiaries. According to Pliny the Elder, they were a breed of ferocious, tawny cattle living in Ethiopia, with mouths gaping open to the ears. Their horns, like a Yale's, could swivel in any direction.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banteng Hutan adalah hewan bestiari kuno dan abad pertengahan; menurut Plinius Tua mereka sapi buas berwarna cokelat kekuningan di Etiopia dengan mulut menganga sampai telinga dan tanduk yang dapat berputar ke segala arah seperti tanduk Yale.",
        "en": "The Forest Bull is an animal of ancient and medieval bestiaries; according to Pliny the Elder they were fierce, tawny cattle of Ethiopia with mouths gaping to the ears and horns that, like a Yale's, could swivel any way."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "forest-bull-c02",
      "source_id": "forest-bull-s1",
      "quote": "According to Aelian, they were called \"flesh eaters\" and were the most ferocious and savage of all animals. They were twice the size of regular bulls and could run at great speeds.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Aelianus mereka disebut \"pemakan daging\", hewan paling buas, dua kali lebih besar dari banteng biasa, dan sangat cepat berlari.",
        "en": "According to Aelian they were called 'flesh eaters', the fiercest of all animals, twice the size of ordinary bulls and very fast."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "forest-bull-c03",
      "source_id": "forest-bull-s1",
      "quote": "No weapon could physically hurt the bulls, be it spears or arrows because even iron deflected from their skin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tak ada senjata yang dapat melukai mereka, karena tombak, panah, bahkan besi memantul dari kulitnya.",
        "en": "No weapon could hurt them, as spears, arrows and even iron glanced off their hide."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "forest-bull-c04",
      "source_id": "forest-bull-s1",
      "quote": "To protect their flocks, herdsmen who lived in the area dug deep ditches, in which the bulls would fall and consequently choke on their rage.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk melindungi ternak, para gembala menggali parit dalam tempat banteng itu jatuh dan mati tercekik oleh amarahnya sendiri.",
        "en": "To protect their flocks, local herdsmen dug deep ditches into which the bulls fell and choked on their own rage."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "forest-bull-c05",
      "source_id": "forest-bull-s2",
      "quote": "Mogelijk is dit dier gebaseerd op reisverslagen die de Afrikaanse buffel beschreven.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Belanda, hewan ini mungkin berdasarkan catatan perjalanan tentang kerbau Afrika.",
        "en": "According to the Dutch article, the animal may be based on travellers' accounts of the African buffalo."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Ga-gorib adalah monster dalam mitologi Khoikhoi yang menantang orang melemparinya batu dari tepi lubang raksasa sampai dikalahkan Heitsi-Eibib.",
    "en": "Ga-gorib is a monster of Khoikhoi mythology who dared people to stone him at the edge of a huge pit until Heitsi-Eibib defeated him.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ga-gorib, \"yang menjatuhkan\", adalah monster dalam mitologi Khoikhoi. Ia duduk di tepi lubang raksasa dan menantang orang yang lewat untuk melemparinya batu, tetapi batu itu selalu memantul, mengenai pelempar, dan menjatuhkannya ke lubang. Pahlawan Heitsi-Eibib menunggu sampai ia memalingkan pandangan, melemparinya tepat di bawah telinga, dan mendorongnya ke dalam lubang.",
      "en": "Ga-gorib, 'the thrower-down', is a monster of Khoikhoi mythology. He sat at the edge of a huge pit and dared passers-by to stone him, but the stones always bounced back, struck the thrower and knocked him into the pit. The hero Heitsi-Eibib waited until he looked away, hit him just under the ear and pushed him into the pit.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ga-gorib-c01",
      "source_id": "ga-gorib-s1",
      "quote": "Ga-gorib (letteralmente \"colui che butta giù\") era un mostro di cui si racconta nella mitologia degli ottentotti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ga-gorib (\"yang menjatuhkan\") adalah monster dalam mitologi orang Khoikhoi (dalam sumber disebut ottentotti).",
        "en": "Ga-gorib ('the thrower-down') was a monster in the mythology of the Khoikhoi (called ottentotti in the source)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ga-gorib-c02",
      "source_id": "ga-gorib-s1",
      "quote": "Di lui si racconta che viveva sull'orlo di un pozzo enorme, chiunque giungeva veniva sfidato da lui: dovevano gettragli contro delle pietre, solo che contro il mostro le pietre gettate tornavano indietro e colpiti cadevano nel pozzo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tinggal di tepi lubang raksasa dan menantang setiap orang yang lewat untuk melemparinya dengan batu, tetapi batu itu memantul kembali, mengenai pelempar, dan menjatuhkannya ke lubang.",
        "en": "It lived on the edge of a huge pit and challenged everyone who came to throw stones at it, but the stones bounced back, struck the thrower and knocked him into the pit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ga-gorib-c03",
      "source_id": "ga-gorib-s1",
      "quote": "L'eroe Heitsi-Eibib giunse per ucciderlo",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pahlawan Heitsi-Eibib datang untuk membunuhnya.",
        "en": "The hero Heitsi-Eibib came to kill it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ga-gorib-c04",
      "source_id": "ga-gorib-s2",
      "quote": "Ga-goribin kukisti lopulta sankari Heitsi-Ebib, joka malttoi odottaa ja heittää kiven vasta sitten, kun hirviö oli kääntänyt katseensa pois. Hän osui Ga-goribia korvan alle ja työnsi hänet alas kuoppaan.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Finlandia, Heitsi-Eibib menunggu sampai monster itu memalingkan pandangan, lalu melempar batu yang mengenai bawah telinganya dan mendorongnya ke dalam lubang.",
        "en": "According to the Finnish article, Heitsi-Eibib waited until the monster looked away, then threw a stone that hit it under the ear and pushed it into the pit."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ganipote adalah manusia pengubah wujud yang terkait sihir dalam cerita rakyat Prancis barat, yang melompat ke punggung pejalan pada malam hari dan mencekiknya.",
    "en": "The ganipote is a witchcraft-linked human shapeshifter of the folklore of western France that leaps onto night travellers' backs and smothers them.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ganipota-c01",
      "ganipota-c03",
      "ganipota-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ganipote atau galipote adalah makhluk jahat dari cerita rakyat Prancis barat-tengah dan barat daya, juga dikenal sampai Quebec dan Republik Dominika. Ia terkait sihir, menghantui hutan gelap pada malam tanpa bulan, dan mengintai rumah sebelum fajar dan sesudah senja. Ia melompat ke punggung pejalan dengan cakar terbuka dan menekan sampai mereka mati tercekik.",
      "en": "The ganipote or galipote is an evil creature of the folklore of central-western and south-western France, also known as far as Quebec and the Dominican Republic. Tied to witchcraft, it haunts dark woods on moonless nights and prowls round houses before dawn and after dusk. It leaps onto passers-by's backs, claws out, and bears down until they suffocate.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ganipota-c01",
        "ganipota-c02",
        "ganipota-c03",
        "ganipota-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam hampir semua kisah ia manusia yang untuk sementara menjadi hewan pada malam hari: serigala, domba, anjing, kambing, kucing, kuda, kelelawar besar, atau burung besar. Tokoh serupa adalah garache, leberon, dan tac.",
      "en": "In almost every tale it is a human temporarily turned into an animal at night: a wolf, sheep, dog, goat, cat, horse, big bat or large bird. Similar figures are the garache, the leberon and the tac.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ganipota-c04",
        "ganipota-c05",
        "ganipota-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "publisher": "Wikipedia (ca)",
      "published": null,
