    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c01",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s1",
      "quote": "La ganipote (parfois orthographiée galipote ou galipotte) est une créature légendaire et maléfique issue du folklore et des légendes des provinces du centre-ouest et du sud-ouest de la France.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ganipote (kadang ditulis galipote atau galipotte) adalah makhluk legendaris jahat dari cerita rakyat provinsi-provinsi Prancis barat-tengah dan barat daya.",
        "en": "The ganipote (sometimes spelled galipote or galipotte) is a legendary evil creature from the folklore of the central-western and south-western provinces of France."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c02",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s1",
      "quote": "Créature malfaisante et protéiforme liée au monde de la sorcellerie, elle hanterait les bois sombres, parcourrait la campagne les nuits sans lune ou rôderait autour des demeures habitées le matin avant le jour, et le soir après le coucher du soleil.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk jahat yang dapat berubah wujud dan terkait dunia sihir ini menghantui hutan gelap, berkeliaran di pedesaan pada malam tanpa bulan, atau mengintai rumah sebelum fajar dan setelah matahari terbenam.",
        "en": "This evil, shape-shifting creature tied to witchcraft haunts dark woods, roams the countryside on moonless nights, or prowls around houses before dawn and after sunset."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c03",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s1",
      "quote": "s'amuse à terroriser les passants en leur sautant violemment sur le dos, toutes griffes dehors. Elle s'y agrippe et pèse de tout son poids jusqu'à ce qu'ils périssent étouffés.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia suka meneror pejalan dengan melompat keras ke punggung mereka dengan cakar terbuka, mencengkeram dan menekan dengan seluruh bobotnya sampai mereka mati tercekik.",
        "en": "It loves terrorising passers-by by leaping violently onto their backs, claws out, clinging and bearing down with all its weight until they suffocate."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c04",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s1",
      "quote": "Mais elle peut prendre la forme d'animaux très variés : moutons, chiens, chèvres, lièvres, porcs, ânes, chats, chevaux, bœufs, mais aussi de grandes chauves-souris ou de grands oiseaux semblables à des oies ou des cygnes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Selain serigala, ia dapat berwujud domba, anjing, kambing, kelinci, babi, keledai, kucing, kuda, lembu, kelelawar besar, atau burung besar seperti angsa.",
        "en": "Besides a wolf, it can take the form of sheep, dogs, goats, hares, pigs, donkeys, cats, horses, oxen, big bats or large birds like geese or swans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c05",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s1",
      "quote": "Dans presque tous les récits, la ganipote est un être humain transformé temporairement en animal ou s'étant d'une certaine façon incarné en animal pendant la nuit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam hampir semua kisah, ganipote adalah manusia yang untuk sementara berubah menjadi hewan atau menjelma ke tubuh hewan pada malam hari.",
        "en": "In almost every tale the ganipote is a human temporarily turned into an animal, or somehow embodied in one at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c06",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s2",
      "quote": "Es tracta d'un personatge ben conegut a la regió francesa del Poitou-Charentes (Aunis, Saintonge, Angoumois, Poitou), a les veïnes Turena i País Gavai, a Occitània (la Guiena gascona i Alvèrnia) i en llocs més allunyats: Forez (on se l'anomena galipoto), Morvan i fins i tot el Quebec i la República Dominicana (el galipote o lugarú).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh ini dikenal di Poitou-Charentes, Touraine, Occitania, dan tempat yang lebih jauh seperti Forez, Morvan, bahkan Quebec dan Republik Dominika (galipote atau lugarú).",
        "en": "The figure is well known in Poitou-Charentes, Touraine, Occitania, and further afield in Forez, the Morvan, even Quebec and the Dominican Republic (the galipote or lugarú)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ganipota-c07",
      "source_id": "ganipota-s2",
      "quote": "Hi ha personatges amb característiques molt similars a les llegendes del Poitou (el garache), el Perigord (el leberon) i les Landes de Gascunya (el tac).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada tokoh dengan ciri sangat mirip dalam legenda Poitou (garache), Périgord (leberon), dan Landes (tac).",
        "en": "There are very similar figures in the legends of Poitou (the garache), Périgord (the leberon) and the Landes (the tac)."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Gigiàt adalah hewan raksasa mirip ibex dan chamois dari Val Masino, Lombardia, penjaga gunung yang sesekali memangsa pendaki sendirian.",
    "en": "The Gigiàt is a giant ibex-chamois-like beast of the Val Masino in Lombardy, a mountain guardian that occasionally eats lone hikers.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "gigiatt-c01",
      "gigiatt-c02",

    {
      "id": "Gigiàt adalah monster raksasa dalam tradisi Alpen Val Masino, Lombardia. Ia mungkin persilangan ibex, rusa, dan chamois berbulu panjang, begitu cepat dan besar sehingga dapat menyeberangi lembah dengan beberapa lompatan. Pada dasarnya ia pemakan tumbuhan, tetapi sesekali memangsa pendaki yang sendirian. Namanya dalam dialek Valtellina berarti \"benda aneh\".",
      "en": "The Gigiàt is a huge monster of the Alpine traditions of the Val Masino in Lombardy. It is probably a cross of ibex, deer and long-haired chamois, so fast and big that it crosses a valley in a few bounds. Basically herbivorous, it now and then eats lone hikers. Its name in the Valtellina dialect means 'strange thing'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "gigiatt-c01",
        "gigiatt-c02",
        "gigiatt-c04",
        "gigiatt-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia berperan sebagai penjaga gunung dan hakim atas perbuatan orang yang melintas, dan sosoknya mirip Dahu.",

      "author": null,

      "published": null,
      "language": "lmo",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c01",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s1",
      "quote": "Il Gigiàt o Gigiàtt sarebbe, nelle tradizioni folkloristiche alpine lombarde, nello specifico della Val Masino, un mostro di proporzioni enormi, che ha la consuetudine di integrare la sua dieta, fondamentalmente vegetariana, con qualche pasto a base di escursionisti o alpinisti solitari",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi rakyat Alpen Lombardia, khususnya Val Masino, Gigiàt adalah monster raksasa yang pada dasarnya pemakan tumbuhan tetapi sesekali memakan pendaki yang berjalan sendirian.",
        "en": "In the Alpine folk traditions of Lombardy, specifically the Val Masino, the Gigiàt is a huge monster whose basically vegetarian diet is supplemented now and then with lone hikers or climbers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c02",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s1",
      "quote": "Probabilmente è un incrocio fra uno stambecco, un cervo ed un camoscio dal pelo lunghissimo, una velocità impressionante e dalle dimensioni pantagruelliche, tanto da poter attraversare un'intera valle con pochi balzi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia mungkin persilangan ibex, rusa, dan chamois berbulu sangat panjang, sangat cepat, dan begitu besar sehingga dapat menyeberangi lembah dengan beberapa lompatan.",
        "en": "It is probably a cross between an ibex, a deer and a chamois, with very long hair, astonishing speed and such vast size that it can cross a whole valley in a few bounds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c03",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s1",
      "quote": "La sua figura ricorda molto quella di un altro animale montano leggendario europeo: il Dahu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sosoknya sangat mirip hewan gunung legendaris Eropa lain, Dahu.",
        "en": "Its figure closely recalls another legendary European mountain animal, the Dahu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c04",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s1",
      "quote": "Già dal nome si comprende l'incertezza delle sue caratteristiche, difatti in dialetto valtellinese gigiàtt significa 'cosa strana/indefinita'.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam dialek Valtellina, gigiàtt berarti \"benda aneh atau tak jelas\", mencerminkan ketidakpastian ciri-cirinya.",
        "en": "In the Valtellina dialect gigiàtt means 'strange or undefined thing', reflecting the uncertainty of its features."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c05",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s1",
      "quote": "Esso possiede la funzione di guardiano di quelle lande e di conseguenza giudice delle azioni di chi le abita o ci transita.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berperan sebagai penjaga daerah itu dan hakim atas perbuatan orang yang tinggal atau melintas di sana.",
        "en": "It acts as guardian of those lands and so as judge of the deeds of those who live in or pass through them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gigiatt-c06",
      "source_id": "gigiatt-s2",
      "quote": "El gh'ha la forma d'on gross cavron, forsi incrosaa cont on camozz, e l'è cognossuu per el sò vess bell svelt e grand.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Lombard, ia berwujud kambing jantan besar, mungkin persilangan dengan chamois, dan dikenal cepat serta besar.",
        "en": "According to the Lombard article, it is shaped like a big billy goat, perhaps crossed with a chamois, and is known for its speed and size."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Hada adalah peri perempuan malam dari Pyrenees Gascogne yang tinggal di gua dan dekat mata air, mirip laminak Basque.",
    "en": "The hada is a nocturnal female fairy of the Gascon Pyrenees living in caves and near springs, akin to the Basque laminak.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "hada-c01",
      "hada-c02",
      "hada-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Hada, \"peri\", dari bahasa Latin fatum, \"takdir\", adalah makhluk perempuan legendaris Pyrenees Gascogne, kadang disebut démaïselas atau damas. Mereka terutama makhluk malam yang tinggal di bawah tanah, di gua, atau dekat mata air, dan banyak nama tempat seperti Hont de las hadas mengenang mereka. Hadetta kadang digambarkan sebagai perempuan berkaki selaput.",
      "en": "The hada, 'fairy', from Latin fatum, 'fate', is a legendary female being of the Gascon Pyrenees, sometimes called démaïselas or damas. Mainly nocturnal, they live underground, in caves or near springs, and many place names like Hont de las hadas recall them. A hadetta is sometimes described as a woman with webbed feet.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "hada-c01",
        "hada-c02",
        "hada-c03",
        "hada-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hada mirip laminak Basque, mora di Aragon Atas, dan dona d'aigua Katalan, yang juga dikaitkan dengan gua dan roh bawah tanah.",
      "en": "The hada is akin to the Basque laminak, the mora of Upper Aragon and the Catalan dona d'aigua, also associated with caves and underground spirits.",

        "hada-c04",
        "hada-c06"

      "title": "Hada (mitologia gascona)",
      "author": null,

      "published": null,
      "language": "ca",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "hada-c01",
      "source_id": "hada-s1",
      "quote": "Une hada (ou hade, c'est-à-dire une fée, du latin fatum : destin) est un être féminin légendaire des Pyrénées gasconnes que l’on appelle parfois aussi  « démaïselas » (demoiselles) ou « damas » (dames).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hada (atau hade, \"peri\", dari bahasa Latin fatum, \"takdir\") adalah makhluk perempuan legendaris dari Pyrenees Gascogne, kadang disebut démaïselas (nona) atau damas (nyonya).",
        "en": "A hada (or hade, 'fairy', from Latin fatum, 'fate') is a legendary female being of the Gascon Pyrenees, sometimes called démaïselas (damsels) or damas (ladies)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hada-c02",
      "source_id": "hada-s1",
      "quote": "Créatures essentiellement nocturnes, les hadas vivent sous terre, dans des grottes ou auprès des sources et des ruisseaux.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hada terutama makhluk malam yang tinggal di bawah tanah, di gua, atau dekat mata air dan sungai kecil.",
        "en": "Mainly nocturnal, hadas live underground, in caves or near springs and streams."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hada-c03",
      "source_id": "hada-s1",
      "quote": "De nombreux lieux dans toutes les Pyrénées et la Gascogne sont associés à leur présence supposée, tout particulièrement le nom de grottes ou de sources, comme le terme « Hont de las hadas » (Fontaine des fées), « la tutera deras hadas » (la grotte des fées)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banyak tempat di Pyrenees dan Gascogne dikaitkan dengan mereka, terutama nama gua dan mata air seperti Hont de las hadas (mata air peri) dan la tutera deras hadas (gua peri).",
        "en": "Many places in the Pyrenees and Gascony are linked to them, especially cave and spring names like Hont de las hadas (fairies' spring) and la tutera deras hadas (fairies' cave)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hada-c04",
      "source_id": "hada-s1",
      "quote": "Les hades s'apparentent aux laminak, très présentes dans la mythologie basque, également associées aux grottes et aux esprits souterrains.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hada mirip laminak dalam mitologi Basque, yang juga dikaitkan dengan gua dan roh bawah tanah.",
        "en": "Hadas are akin to the laminak of Basque mythology, also associated with caves and underground spirits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hada-c05",
      "source_id": "hada-s1",
      "quote": "L’imagerie populaire et la mythologie pyrénéenne décrivent aussi parfois une « hadetta » comme une femme aux pieds palmés.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitologi Pyrenees kadang menggambarkan \"hadetta\" sebagai perempuan berkaki selaput.",
        "en": "Pyrenean imagery sometimes describes a 'hadetta' as a woman with webbed feet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hada-c06",
      "source_id": "hada-s2",
      "quote": "L'hada gascona, en canvi, té molt més a veure amb la lamina basca, anomenada mora a l'Alt Aragó, o la dona d'aigua catalana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, hada Gascogne lebih dekat dengan lamina Basque, mora di Aragon Atas, atau dona d'aigua Katalan.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, the Gascon hada is much closer to the Basque lamina, called mora in Upper Aragon, or the Catalan dona d'aigua."
      }
    }
  ],

{
  "schema": "mythics-entry/1",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Hypnalis adalah ular bestiari abad pertengahan yang membunuh korbannya dalam tidur tanpa bangun, konon ular yang dipakai Cleopatra.",
    "en": "The hypnalis is a medieval bestiary serpent that kills its victims in an endless sleep, said to be Cleopatra's asp.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "hypnalis-c01",
      "hypnalis-c02",
      "hypnalis-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Hypnalis atau ipnale, dari kata Yunani hypnos, \"tidur\", adalah ular legendaris dalam bestiari abad pertengahan. Bisanya membunuh dengan menenggelamkan korban ke dalam tidur tanpa bangun; menurut versi lain ia menyerang orang yang tidur karena bergerak tanpa suara. Tradisi menyebut ular aspis Cleopatra adalah hypnalis, dan Boccaccio menyebutnya dalam komentarnya tentang Divina Commedia.",
      "en": "The hypnalis or ipnale, from Greek hypnos, 'sleep', is a legendary serpent of medieval bestiaries. Its venom kills by sinking victims into a sleep without waking; in other versions it strikes sleepers because it moves silently. Tradition says Cleopatra's asp was a hypnalis, and Boccaccio names it in his commentary on the Divine Comedy.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "hypnalis-c01",
        "hypnalis-c02",
        "hypnalis-c03",
        "hypnalis-c04",
        "hypnalis-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "hypnalis-c01",
      "source_id": "hypnalis-s1",
      "quote": "L'ipnale (in latino ypnalis o ipnalis; dal greco ὕπνος, hypnos, \"sonno\") è un serpente leggendario descritto nei bestiari medievali.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ipnale (bahasa Latin ypnalis atau ipnalis, dari bahasa Yunani hypnos, \"tidur\") adalah ular legendaris dalam bestiari abad pertengahan.",
        "en": "The ipnale (Latin ypnalis or ipnalis, from Greek hypnos, 'sleep') is a legendary serpent of medieval bestiaries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hypnalis-c02",
      "source_id": "hypnalis-s1",
      "quote": "In particolare, il veleno dell'ipnale causa la morte facendo sprofondare le sue vittime in un sonno senza risveglio (da cui il nome).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bisa ipnale membunuh dengan menenggelamkan korbannya ke dalam tidur tanpa bangun lagi, asal namanya.",
        "en": "Its venom kills by plunging victims into a sleep from which they never wake, hence its name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hypnalis-c03",
      "source_id": "hypnalis-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo altre versioni, il sonno non sarebbe l'effetto del veleno del serpente, bensì il momento che questo sceglie per attaccare le sue vittime: l'ipnale si muove infatti silenziosamente.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut versi lain, tidur bukan efek bisanya, melainkan saat yang dipilihnya untuk menyerang, karena ia bergerak tanpa suara.",
        "en": "In other versions sleep is not the venom's effect but the moment it chooses to strike, since it moves silently."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hypnalis-c04",
      "source_id": "hypnalis-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo la tradizione il famoso aspide di Cleopatra, dal quale ella si fece mordere per suicidarsi, era proprio un ipnale.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi, ular aspis terkenal yang dipakai Cleopatra untuk bunuh diri adalah ipnale.",
        "en": "Tradition holds that Cleopatra's famous asp, by which she had herself bitten to die, was a hypnalis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hypnalis-c05",
      "source_id": "hypnalis-s1",
      "quote": "Anche Boccaccio, nel suo commento alla Divina commedia, richiama esplicitamente l'ipnale nel riferirsi a questo episodio.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Boccaccio juga menyebut ipnale secara eksplisit dalam komentarnya tentang Divina Commedia saat membahas peristiwa itu.",
        "en": "Boccaccio too names the ipnale explicitly in his commentary on the Divine Comedy when discussing this episode."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "El Familiar adalah roh berwujud anjing hitam raksasa dari pabrik gula Argentina barat laut yang memberi kekayaan kepada pemilik pabrik dengan imbalan nyawa pekerja.",
    "en": "El Familiar is a huge black dog spirit of the sugar mills of north-western Argentina that brings mill owners wealth in exchange for workers' lives.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kaka-c01",
      "kaka-c02",
      "kaka-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "El Familiar adalah roh pendamping pemakan manusia yang legendanya tersebar di pabrik gula Argentina barat laut. Ia paling sering digambarkan sebagai anjing hitam raksasa setinggi manusia dengan mata menyala, dan pada malam hari dapat menjadi ular yang melingkari barak pekerja. Ia memberi kekuasaan dan uang kepada pemiliknya dengan imbalan nyawa manusia.",
      "en": "El Familiar is a man-eating familiar spirit whose legend is widespread in the sugar mills of north-western Argentina. It is most often a huge black dog as tall as a man with blazing eyes, and at night it can become a snake coiling round the workers' sheds. It gives its owner power and money in exchange for human lives.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kaka-c01",
        "kaka-c02",
        "kaka-c05",
        "kaka-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Legenda ini sering melibatkan perjanjian pemilik pabrik dengan Iblis, dibayar dengan nyawa pekerja; Ingenio Santa Ana di Tucumán disebut yang pertama memilikinya. Hilangnya para pekerja di masa kediktatoran pun dikaitkan dengan El Familiar.",
      "en": "The legend often involves a pact between mill owners and the Devil, paid for with workers' lives; the Ingenio Santa Ana in Tucumán is said to have been the first to have one. Workers' disappearances under the dictatorship were also blamed on El Familiar.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kaka-c03",
        "kaka-c04",
        "kaka-c07"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kaka-c01",
      "source_id": "kaka-s1",
      "quote": "El Familiar, también conocido como El cuñado, El Tío o Perro Familiar​ es un tipo de espíritu familiar devorador de hombres, cuya leyenda se halla muy difundida en los ingenios azucareros del noroeste argentino.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "El Familiar, dikenal juga sebagai El cuñado, El Tío, atau Perro Familiar, adalah sejenis roh pendamping pemakan manusia yang legendanya tersebar luas di pabrik gula Argentina barat laut.",
        "en": "El Familiar, also known as El cuñado, El Tío or Perro Familiar, is a man-eating familiar spirit whose legend is widespread in the sugar mills of north-western Argentina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c02",
      "source_id": "kaka-s1",
      "quote": "Se han descrito diferentes versiones de esta entidad, pero la más conocida lo identifica con una especie de criatura demoníaca con aspecto de cánido que da poder y dinero a su poseedor a cambio, generalmente, de vidas humanas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Versi paling terkenal menggambarkannya sebagai makhluk iblis berwujud anjing yang memberi kekuasaan dan uang kepada pemiliknya dengan imbalan, biasanya, nyawa manusia.",
        "en": "The best-known version makes it a demonic dog-like creature that gives its owner power and money in exchange, usually, for human lives."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c03",
      "source_id": "kaka-s1",
      "quote": "A veces, la leyenda involucra un pacto entre los dueños de los ingenios y el Diablo, que asegura la prevalencia de su estatus socioeconómico a cambio de sus almas y de la vida de algunos de sus trabajadores, arrebatadas periódicamente por el Familiar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang legenda ini melibatkan perjanjian antara pemilik pabrik gula dan Iblis yang menjamin kedudukan mereka dengan imbalan jiwa mereka dan nyawa sebagian pekerja, yang diambil El Familiar secara berkala.",
        "en": "Sometimes the legend involves a pact between the mill owners and the Devil, securing their status in exchange for their souls and the lives of some workers, taken periodically by the Familiar."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c04",
      "source_id": "kaka-s1",
      "quote": "Muchas desapariciones de zafreros del Ingenio Ledesma y otros sucedidas durante el Proceso de Reorganización Nacional han sido vinculadas con el accionar del Familiar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banyak hilangnya pekerja tebu di Ingenio Ledesma dan pabrik lain pada masa kediktatoran Proceso de Reorganización Nacional dikaitkan dengan El Familiar.",
        "en": "Many disappearances of cane workers at the Ingenio Ledesma and other mills during the Proceso de Reorganización Nacional dictatorship have been linked to the Familiar."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c05",
      "source_id": "kaka-s2",
      "quote": "La forma física més difosa d'aquest ésser monstruós és com un gos negre i enorme, que fins i tot pot arribar a l'altura d'un home. Té una mirada refulgent, fins a arribar a llançar foc pels seus ulls, segons alguns relats.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wujudnya yang paling umum adalah anjing hitam raksasa setinggi manusia, bermata menyala yang menurut sebagian kisah menyemburkan api.",
        "en": "Its most common form is a huge black dog as tall as a man, with blazing eyes that in some accounts shoot fire."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c06",
      "source_id": "kaka-s2",
      "quote": "De nit, pot adoptar la forma d'una serp, voltant d'aquesta manera els coberts on dormen els treballadors.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada malam hari ia dapat berwujud ular dan melingkari barak tempat para pekerja tidur.",
        "en": "At night it can take the form of a snake, coiling around the sheds where the workers sleep."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kaka-c07",
      "source_id": "kaka-s2",
      "quote": "S'atribueix a l'Ingenio Santa Ana de Tucumán, propietat de Clodomiro Hileret, d'haver estat el primer a aconseguir el seu propi Familiar",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ingenio Santa Ana di Tucumán milik Clodomiro Hileret disebut sebagai yang pertama memiliki El Familiar sendiri.",
        "en": "The Ingenio Santa Ana in Tucumán, owned by Clodomiro Hileret, is said to have been the first to obtain its own Familiar."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Karnabo adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Ardennes yang berhidung belalai dan bermata basilisk, yang napasnya melumpuhkan dan tinggal di tambang sabak.",
    "en": "The Karnabo is a creature of Ardennes folklore with a trunk for a nose and basilisk eyes, whose breath paralyses and who lives in a slate quarry.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "karnabo-c01",
      "karnabo-c02",
      "karnabo-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Karnabo adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Ardennes, keturunan iblis atau ghoul dan seorang pengembara Bohemia yang mewariskan kekuatannya, termasuk membuat mantra dan menyembuhkan kurap kuku pada Jumat Agung. Ia mirip manusia tetapi berhidung belalai gajah, bermata kuning basilisk, dan berkulit gelap kasar; napas dan siulannya melumpuhkan manusia dan membunuh hewan.",
      "en": "The Karnabo is a creature of Ardennes folklore, offspring of a demon or ghoul and a Bohemian traveller who passed on his powers, including spell-making and curing whitlow on Good Friday. It looks like a man but has an elephant's trunk, yellow basilisk eyes and dark rough skin; its breath and whistle paralyse people and kill animals.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "karnabo-c01",
        "karnabo-c02",
        "karnabo-c04",
        "karnabo-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia lama tinggal di tambang sabak terbengkalai dekat Regniowez dan konon menculik seorang gadis; saat badai rintihan gadis itu dan lengkingan Karnabo masih terdengar.",
      "en": "It long lived in an abandoned slate quarry near Regniowez and is said to have abducted a girl; during storms her moans and the Karnabo's trumpeting can still be heard.",

      "language": "fr",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c01",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s1",
      "quote": "The Karnabo is a creature of Ardennes folklore.  It is reputedly the offspring of a supernatural entity (either a demon or a ghoul) and a Bohemian traveller (sometimes said to be a sorcerer).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karnabo adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Ardennes, konon keturunan makhluk gaib (iblis atau ghoul) dan seorang pengembara Bohemia (kadang disebut penyihir).",
        "en": "The Karnabo is a creature of Ardennes folklore, said to be the offspring of a supernatural being (a demon or ghoul) and a Bohemian traveller (sometimes called a sorcerer)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c02",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s1",
      "quote": "The Karnabo resembles a man but with an elephant's trunk for a nose and the eyes of a basilisk.  The breath of the creature is said to be able to paralyse men and kill animals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karnabo mirip manusia tetapi berhidung belalai gajah dan bermata basilisk; napasnya konon dapat melumpuhkan manusia dan membunuh hewan.",
        "en": "The Karnabo looks like a man but with an elephant's trunk for a nose and a basilisk's eyes; its breath can paralyse people and kill animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c03",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s1",
      "quote": "It is said to have abducted a young girl and folklore states that her moans and trumpet of the creature can be heard during thunderstorms.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon menculik seorang gadis, dan saat badai petir rintihan gadis itu dan lengkingan makhluk itu masih terdengar.",
        "en": "It is said to have abducted a young girl, and her moans and the creature's trumpeting can be heard during thunderstorms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c04",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s1",
      "quote": "Before leaving the area for good he passed some of his powers onto the Karnabo, including the making of spells and the ability to cure whitlow on Good Friday.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebelum pergi, sang Bohemia mewariskan sebagian kekuatannya kepada Karnabo, termasuk membuat mantra dan menyembuhkan kurap kuku pada Jumat Agung.",
        "en": "Before leaving for good, the Bohemian passed some powers to the Karnabo, including spell-making and curing whitlow on Good Friday."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c05",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s1",
      "quote": "By one account it resided for a long time in an abandoned slate quarry near Regniowez.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut satu kisah ia lama tinggal di tambang batu sabak terbengkalai dekat Regniowez.",
        "en": "By one account it lived for a long time in an abandoned slate quarry near Regniowez."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karnabo-c06",
      "source_id": "karnabo-s2",
      "quote": "Il a une tête presque humaine, mais possède une trompe de laquelle sort son sifflement strident, des yeux jaune de basilic et une peau sombre et rugueuse comme les parois de l'ardoisière ou il vit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, kepalanya hampir manusia, tetapi berbelalai yang mengeluarkan siulan melengking, bermata kuning basilisk, dan berkulit gelap kasar seperti dinding tambang sabak tempatnya tinggal.",
        "en": "According to the French article, its head is almost human, but it has a trunk that gives out a shrill whistle, yellow basilisk eyes and dark, rough skin like the walls of its slate quarry."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kuusu atau Khuso adalah orang-orangan sawah penangkal roh jahat di Negeri Basque, juga nama roh malam jahil yang setara dengan iratxo.",
    "en": "The Kuusu or Khuso is a scarecrow against evil spirits in the Basque Country, also the name of a mischievous night sprite equated with the iratxo.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kuusu-c01",
      "kuusu-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Kuusu adalah semacam orang-orangan sawah yang dahulu dipakai di Negeri Basque untuk melawan roh jahat. Mitologi Basque juga mengenal roh Khuso yang menurut Julio Caro Baroja setara dengan iratxo atau etxejaun, peri malam yang jahil. Kamus etimologi Agud dan Tovar mengartikan Khuso sebagai \"orang-orangan sawah, hantu\", pinjaman dari kata Béarn gusot.",
      "en": "The Kuusu was a kind of scarecrow formerly used in the Basque Country against evil spirits. Basque mythology also knows a spirit called Khuso which Julio Caro Baroja equates with the iratxo or etxejaun, a mischievous night sprite. Agud and Tovar's etymological dictionary defines Khuso as 'scarecrow, ghost', a loan from Béarnese gusot.",
      "claim_ids": [

      ]

    {
      "id": "kuusu-c01",
      "source_id": "kuusu-s1",
      "quote": "El Kuusu era una mena d'espantaocells que es feia servir antigament al País Basc per a combatre els mals esperits.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuusu adalah semacam orang-orangan sawah yang dahulu dipakai di Negeri Basque untuk melawan roh jahat.",
        "en": "The Kuusu was a kind of scarecrow formerly used in the Basque Country to fight evil spirits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuusu-c02",
      "source_id": "kuusu-s1",
      "quote": "D'altra banda, en la mitologia basca hi ha un geni anomenat Khuso que, segons Julio Caro Baroja, és equiparable a l'iratxo, altrament dit idittu o etxejaun, una mena de follet entremaliat nocturn.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitologi Basque juga mengenal roh bernama Khuso yang menurut Julio Caro Baroja setara dengan iratxo, juga disebut idittu atau etxejaun, semacam peri malam yang jahil.",
        "en": "Basque mythology also has a spirit called Khuso which, according to Julio Caro Baroja, equals the iratxo, also called idittu or etxejaun, a kind of mischievous night sprite."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuusu-c03",
      "source_id": "kuusu-s1",
      "quote": "Al Diccionario Etimológico Vasco de Manuel Agud i Antonio Tovar, el mot Khuso hi apareix definit com a «espantaocells, fantasma [..] manlleu del bearnès gusot».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kamus Etimologi Basque karya Manuel Agud dan Antonio Tovar mengartikan Khuso sebagai \"orang-orangan sawah, hantu\", pinjaman dari bahasa Béarn gusot.",
        "en": "Manuel Agud and Antonio Tovar's Basque Etymological Dictionary defines Khuso as 'scarecrow, ghost', a loan from Béarnese gusot."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Leontophone adalah hewan kecil dari tulisan Yunani-Romawi dan bestiari Abad Pertengahan yang begitu beracun sehingga singa yang menggigitnya mati.",
    "en": "The leontophone is a small animal of Greco-Roman writings and Medieval bestiaries so poisonous that a lion biting it dies.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "leontophone-c01",
      "leontophone-c02",
      "leontophone-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Leontophone, dari bahasa Yunani kuno \"pembunuh singa\", adalah hewan kecil yang kemungkinan besar legendaris. Ia pertama dicatat penulis Yunani-Romawi lalu masuk bestiari Abad Pertengahan. Singa yang menggigitnya akan mati; dalam ritual yang tepat, dagingnya dibakar dan abunya ditaburkan pada daging umpan di persimpangan jalan untuk meracuni singa.",
      "en": "The leontophone, Ancient Greek for 'lion-killer', is a small and most probably legendary animal. Greco-Roman writers first recorded it, and it later entered Medieval bestiaries. A lion that bites one dies; in the proper ritual its flesh is burned and the ashes scattered on bait meat at a crossroads to poison lions.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "leontophone-c01",
        "leontophone-c02",
        "leontophone-c03",
        "leontophone-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Singa membenci leontophone dan mencabiknya dengan cakar alih-alih menggigit. Namun menurut sebagian sumber singa justru lari dan hanya menyerang bila terdesak, karena air seninya pun mematikan. Wujudnya tidak pernah digambarkan pasti: babi hutan kecil, beruang kecil, atau ular.",
      "en": "Lions hate the leontophone and rip it with their claws instead of biting. Yet some sources say the lion runs away and fights only when cornered, because even its urine is deadly. Its look is never fixed: a little boar, a little bear, or a serpent.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "leontophone-c05",
        "leontophone-c06",
        "leontophone-c07",
        "leontophone-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c01",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "Leontophone (Leontophonus, Leontophonos, Leophontes, from Ancient Greek: λεοντοφόνος, meaning \"lion-killer\") was a small animal, most probably legendary, that was deadly to lions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Leontophone (Leontophonus, Leontophonos, Leophontes; dari bahasa Yunani Kuno λεοντοφόνος, \"pembunuh singa\") adalah hewan kecil, kemungkinan besar legendaris, yang mematikan bagi singa.",
        "en": "The leontophone (Leontophonus, Leontophonos, Leophontes; from Ancient Greek λεοντοφόνος, 'lion-killer') was a small, most probably legendary animal deadly to lions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c02",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "The first written record is from Greco-Roman writers, but it was also known later during the Medieval period, in Medieval bestiaries.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Catatan tertulis pertamanya berasal dari penulis Yunani-Romawi, dan kemudian ia juga dikenal dalam bestiari Abad Pertengahan.",
        "en": "Its first written record comes from Greco-Roman writers, and it was later known in Medieval bestiaries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c03",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "Leontophones are described as fatally poisonous to lions, such that a lion will die if it merely bites a leontophone.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Leontophone digambarkan sangat beracun bagi singa: singa akan mati hanya dengan menggigitnya.",
        "en": "Leontophones are described as fatally poisonous to lions: a lion dies merely by biting one."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c04",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "In a proper ritual, the leontophone's flesh is burned and its ashes are sprinkled on a piece of meat, which is placed at a crossroad; if a lion eats even a small amount of the tainted meat, it dies.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam ritual yang tepat, daging leontophone dibakar dan abunya ditaburkan pada sepotong daging yang diletakkan di persimpangan jalan; singa yang memakannya sedikit saja akan mati.",
        "en": "In the proper ritual its flesh is burned and the ashes sprinkled on meat left at a crossroads; a lion eating even a little of it dies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c05",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "However, some sources state that the lion will instead flee from the small creature, only attacking and killing it when left with no option to flee (since attacking the leontophone may cause it to urinate, and the urine of the leontophone is also fatally poisonous to lions).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian sumber menyebut singa justru lari darinya dan hanya menyerang bila terdesak, karena air seni leontophone juga mematikan bagi singa.",
        "en": "Some sources say the lion flees instead and attacks only when cornered, since the leontophone's urine is also deadly to lions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c06",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "Likewise, due to the danger they pose to lions, lions instinctively hate leontophones. Confronted by a leontophone, they often hunt and kill them, tearing them apart with their claws rather than biting them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Singa membenci leontophone; bila berhadapan, singa sering memburu dan membunuhnya dengan mencabik memakai cakar, bukan menggigit.",
        "en": "Lions hate leontophones; confronted with one, they often hunt and kill it, tearing it with their claws rather than biting."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c07",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s1",
      "quote": "Though no sources give a definite depiction of the leontophone's appearance, some sources speculate that the creature looks like a small boar or a small bear, while others speculate that it is serpentine",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak ada sumber yang memberi gambaran pasti tentang wujudnya; sebagian menduga mirip babi hutan kecil atau beruang kecil, yang lain menduga mirip ular.",
        "en": "No source gives a definite description of its appearance; some guess a small boar or small bear, others that it is serpentine."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "leontophone-c08",
      "source_id": "leontophone-s2",
      "quote": "Leeuwen haten leontophonen. Ze jagen erop en doden hen, eerder door ze te verscheuren met hun klauwen, dan ze te bijten.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Belanda juga menyebut singa membenci leontophone dan memburunya, mencabiknya dengan cakar alih-alih menggigit.",
        "en": "The Dutch article also says lions hate leontophones and hunt them, tearing them with their claws rather than biting."
      }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Longana adalah makhluk air perempuan berkaki kambing dari tradisi Cadore dan Comelico, Italia, yang hidup berkelompok di gua dan dapat meramalkan peristiwa alam.",
    "en": "The Longana is a goat-legged female water being of the tradition of Cadore and Comelico, Italy, who lives in groups in caves and can foretell natural events.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "longana-c01",
      "longana-c02",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Longana, di sebagian tempat disebut Anguana, adalah makhluk air perempuan dari tradisi Cadore dan Comelico, terutama Lozzo di Cadore. Mereka hidup berkelompok di gua dan celah batu yang namanya mengenang mereka, seperti Bùs de le Longane di Vallesella. Mereka perempuan cantik dan cerdas berkaki kambing yang lewat roh hutan dapat meramalkan peristiwa alam.",
      "en": "The Longana, called Anguana in some places, is a female water being of the tradition of Cadore and Comelico, above all Lozzo di Cadore. They live in groups in caves and rock clefts whose names recall them, such as the Bùs de le Longane at Vallesella. They are beautiful, clever goat-legged women who, through the forest spirit, can foresee natural events.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "longana-c01",
        "longana-c02",
        "longana-c03",
        "longana-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam versi Domegge, leluhur keluarga Da Deppo menikahi seorang Longana dengan syarat tak pernah mengungkit asal-usulnya. Ketika ia memanen lebih awal karena meramalkan badai, suaminya mengejeknya \"kaki kambing\", dan ia lenyap ke hutan. Di Comelico ada yang menduga mereka sebenarnya perempuan dari kelompok pendatang Hungaria.",
      "en": "In the Domegge version the ancestor of the Da Deppo family weds a Longana on condition he never mentions her origins. When she harvests early, foreseeing a storm, he mocks her as 'goat-foot' and she vanishes into the woods. In Comelico some think they were really women of a Hungarian migrant group.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "longana-c04",
        "longana-c05",
        "longana-c06",
        "longana-c07"

      "title": "Лонгана (легендарно суштество)",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "longana-c01",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Il nome Longana (o a seconda del luogo, come in Oltrepiave e Auronzo  Anguana) indica una creatura acquatica fantastica di sesso femminile tipica della tradizione del Cadore e Comelico (soprattutto Lozzo di Cadore).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Longana (di beberapa tempat seperti Oltrepiave dan Auronzo disebut Anguana) adalah makhluk air perempuan dari tradisi Cadore dan Comelico, terutama Lozzo di Cadore.",
        "en": "The Longana (in places such as Oltrepiave and Auronzo called Anguana) is a female water creature of the tradition of Cadore and Comelico, especially Lozzo di Cadore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c02",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Nella tradizione questo essere suole vivere in gruppo in grotte o anfratti rocciosi che nella toponimia locale prendono il suo nome: esiste a Vallesella un anfratto detto \"Bùs de le Longane\" e a Lozzo un sasso detto \"Sass de le Longane\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka hidup berkelompok di gua atau celah batu yang dinamai menurut mereka, seperti celah Bùs de le Longane di Vallesella dan batu Sass de le Longane di Lozzo.",
        "en": "They live in groups in caves or rock clefts named after them, like the Bùs de le Longane cleft at Vallesella and the Sass de le Longane rock at Lozzo."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c03",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Sono raffigurate come donne bellissime e molto intelligenti, ma con arti inferiori di capra, e poste dalla loro duplice natura in diretta comunicazione con lo spirito silvestre che conferisce loro poteri straordinari di predizione degli eventi naturali.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka digambarkan sebagai perempuan sangat cantik dan cerdas berkaki kambing, yang karena sifat gandanya berhubungan langsung dengan roh hutan yang memberi mereka kemampuan luar biasa meramalkan peristiwa alam.",
        "en": "They are depicted as very beautiful, intelligent women with goat legs whose dual nature links them directly to the forest spirit, giving them extraordinary power to foretell natural events."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c04",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Nella versione domeggese della leggenda un uomo, capostipite della importante famiglia Da Deppo, si invaghisce di una Longana e la fa sua sposa, a patto che lui non le ricordi mai le sue origini.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi Domegge, seorang lelaki, leluhur keluarga Da Deppo, jatuh cinta pada seorang Longana dan menikahinya dengan syarat ia tidak pernah mengungkit asal-usul istrinya.",
        "en": "In the Domegge version a man, ancestor of the Da Deppo family, falls for a Longana and marries her on condition he never reminds her of her origins."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c05",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Dopo anni prosperi, in seguito a una mietitura precoce del grano da parte della Longana che aveva previsto una tempesta, l'uomo la insulta chiamandola \"piè di capra\" e la Longana sparisce nei boschi lasciando i figli nati dall'unione.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah bertahun-tahun makmur, Longana memanen gandum lebih awal karena meramalkan badai; suaminya menghinanya dengan sebutan \"kaki kambing\", lalu ia menghilang ke hutan meninggalkan anak-anak mereka.",
        "en": "After prosperous years the Longana reaps the wheat early because she foresaw a storm; her husband insults her as 'goat-foot' and she vanishes into the woods, leaving their children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c06",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Nel vicino Comelico alcuni suggeriscono che le stesse fossero in realtà le rappresentanti femminili di una popolazione ungara migrata in quei territori che viveva di legname e pastorizia al margine del bosco",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Comelico, sebagian orang menduga mereka sebenarnya perempuan dari suatu kelompok Hungaria yang bermigrasi ke sana dan hidup dari kayu serta penggembalaan di tepi hutan.",
        "en": "In nearby Comelico some suggest they were really the women of a Hungarian group that migrated there and lived by timber and herding at the forest's edge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c07",
      "source_id": "longana-s1",
      "quote": "Nella leggenda delle Longane appaiono molti \"topos\" delle tradizioni orali: l'ambiguità della donna, la natura silvana mezzo umano/mezzo animale, i rituali di purificazione nei bagni notturni che solevano praticare queste creature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda Longana memuat banyak topos tradisi lisan: ambiguitas perempuan, sifat hutan setengah manusia setengah hewan, dan ritual penyucian berupa mandi malam yang biasa mereka lakukan.",
        "en": "The Longana legend contains many oral-tradition topoi: the woman's ambiguity, the half-human half-animal woodland nature, and the purification rituals of night bathing these creatures practised."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "longana-c08",
      "source_id": "longana-s2",
      "quote": "Лонгана е легендарно водно суштество од женски род. Лонганите се споменуваат во легендите за луѓето од Кадоре, Италија.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Makedonia juga menyebut Longana makhluk air legendaris perempuan dalam legenda orang Cadore, Italia.",
        "en": "The Macedonian article also calls the Longana a legendary female water being in the legends of the people of Cadore, Italy."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Macrâle adalah penyihir perempuan dalam cerita rakyat Wallonia, Flandria, dan Ardennes, yang dituduh mendatangkan musim dingin, banjir, dan wabah.",
    "en": "The macrâle is a witch of the folklore of Wallonia, Flanders and the Ardennes, blamed for bringing winter, floods and epidemics.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "macrale-c01",

    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Macrâle, dari kata Walloon Liège dan Luxembourg untuk penyihir perempuan, adalah sosok penyihir dalam cerita rakyat Wallonia, Flandria, dan Ardennes. Menurut Pierre Dubois ia campuran elf dan penyihir yang menyukai sabat dan berkeliling pada malam hari menebar kutukan. Ia dituduh mendatangkan musim dingin, banjir, dan wabah, dan menghuni hutan seperti nuton dan sottai.",
      "en": "The macrâle, from a Liège and Luxembourg Walloon word for a witch, is a witch figure of the folklore of Wallonia, Flanders and the Ardennes. Pierre Dubois calls her a mix of elf and witch who loves sabbaths and goes about at night spreading curses. She is blamed for bringing winter, floods and epidemics, and dwells in the forests like the nutons and sottai.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "macrale-c01",
        "macrale-c02",
        "macrale-c03",
        "macrale-c04",
        "macrale-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kini banyak kelompok folklor memerankan macrâle dalam sabat dan pawai, misalnya di Haccourt, Vielsalm, dan Ellezelles. Kelompok Macralles du val de Salm di Vielsalm didirikan pada 1955 untuk mengenang legenda tokoh nyata Gustine Maka.",
      "en": "Today many folklore groups play macrâles in sabbaths and parades, for example at Haccourt, Vielsalm and Ellezelles. The Macralles du val de Salm group at Vielsalm was set up in 1955 to recall a legend about the real Gustine Maka.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "macrale-c05",
        "macrale-c06"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "La macrâle, makrâlle, macralle, macrale (ou makralle en flamand) désigne une sorcière en Wallonie, en Flandre et dans les Ardennes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Macrâle (juga makrâlle, macralle, macrale, atau makralle dalam bahasa Flam) adalah sebutan untuk penyihir perempuan di Wallonia, Flandria, dan Ardennes.",
        "en": "The macrâle (also makrâlle, macralle, macrale, or makralle in Flemish) is the name for a witch in Wallonia, Flanders and the Ardennes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c02",
      "source_id": "macrale-s1",
      "quote": "Le terme macrâle provient du wallon liégeois et luxembourgeois et a comme masculin le terme macrê qui quant à lui signifie sorcier.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah macrâle berasal dari bahasa Walloon Liège dan Luxembourg; bentuk maskulinnya, macrê, berarti penyihir laki-laki.",
        "en": "The term macrâle comes from the Walloon of Liège and Luxembourg; its masculine form, macrê, means a male sorcerer."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c03",
      "source_id": "macrale-s1",
      "quote": "Selon Pierre Dubois, la macrâle est un mélange d'elfe et de sorcière qui affectionne les sabbats, et se promène durant la nuit en lançant un florilège de maléfices.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Pierre Dubois, macrâle adalah campuran elf dan penyihir yang menyukai sabat dan berkeliaran pada malam hari sambil melontarkan berbagai kutukan.",
        "en": "According to Pierre Dubois, the macrâle is a mix of elf and witch who loves sabbaths and roams at night casting a host of curses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c04",
      "source_id": "macrale-s1",
      "quote": "La macrâle est accusée d'être responsable de bien des maux, tels que la venue de l'hiver, des inondations et des épidémies. Elles peuplent les forêts comme d'autres créatures du folklore belge et ardennais (nutons, sottai, Bayard).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Macrâle dituduh menyebabkan banyak bencana seperti datangnya musim dingin, banjir, dan wabah; mereka menghuni hutan seperti makhluk lain dalam cerita rakyat Belgia dan Ardennes (nuton, sottai, Bayard).",
        "en": "The macrâle is blamed for many ills such as the coming of winter, floods and epidemics; they people the forests like other creatures of Belgian and Ardennes folklore (nutons, sottai, Bayard)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c05",
      "source_id": "macrale-s1",
      "quote": "De nombreux groupes folkloriques représentent des macrales et sorcières, notamment dans les sabbats de Haccourt, Vielsalm, Ellezelles (où elles sont appelées \"chorchîles\")",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banyak kelompok folklor memerankan macrâle dan penyihir, antara lain dalam sabat di Haccourt, Vielsalm, dan Ellezelles (tempat mereka disebut chorchîles).",
        "en": "Many folklore groups portray macrâles and witches, notably in the sabbaths of Haccourt, Vielsalm and Ellezelles (where they are called chorchîles)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c06",
      "source_id": "macrale-s1",
      "quote": "Les Macralles du val de Salm (ou de Vielsam) est un groupe folklorique fondé en 1955 et qui a été créé en l'honneur d'une légende locale, autour de la personnalité bien réelle de Marie-Joseph-Augustine Lemoine dite Gustine Maka.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kelompok folklor Macralles du val de Salm di Vielsalm didirikan pada 1955 untuk menghormati legenda setempat tentang tokoh nyata Marie-Joseph-Augustine Lemoine, yang dijuluki Gustine Maka.",
        "en": "The folklore group Macralles du val de Salm at Vielsalm was founded in 1955 in honour of a local legend about the real person Marie-Joseph-Augustine Lemoine, called Gustine Maka."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "macrale-c07",
      "source_id": "macrale-s2",
      "quote": "De makrallen in het Vlaams, les macrâles, macralles ou makrâlles in het Waals, komt van een Luiks-Waals woord voor heks.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Belanda juga menyebut makrallen (Flam) atau macrâles (Walloon) berasal dari kata Walloon Liège untuk penyihir perempuan.",
        "en": "The Dutch article likewise says makrallen (Flemish) or macrâles (Walloon) comes from a Liège Walloon word for witch."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia fr dan nl; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "claims",
      "searched": "Sebagian besar artikel fr dan nl membahas kelompok folklor dan pawai modern, bukan kepercayaan lama tentang macrâle."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mama 'e su Sole adalah perempuan cantik dalam cerita rakyat Sardinia yang muncul pada siang terpanas musim panas dan membuat anak yang bermain di luar terserang demam.",
    "en": "Mama 'e su Sole is a beautiful woman of Sardinian folklore who appears in the hottest summer afternoons and gives fever to children playing outdoors.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mama-e-su-sole-c01",
      "mama-e-su-sole-c03",
      "mama-e-su-sole-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mama 'e su Sole, \"Ibu Matahari\", dikenal hampir di seluruh Sardinia. Ia perempuan sangat cantik yang muncul pada musim panas dari tengah hari sampai sekitar pukul 16. Bila bertemu anak yang bermain di jalan tanpa izin orang tuanya, ia mengejarnya, menyentuh dahinya hingga berbekas, dan anak itu terserang demam halentura beberapa hari.",
      "en": "Mama 'e su Sole, 'Mother of the Sun', is known almost all over Sardinia. She is a very beautiful woman who appears in summer from noon until about 16:00. If she meets a child playing in the street without its parents' leave, she runs it down, marks its brow with a touch, and the child falls ill with halentura fever for days.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mama-e-su-sole-c01",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c03",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c04",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia dipakai untuk menakuti anak agar tidak keluar saat terik dan juga menjelaskan demam akibat sengatan matahari, mirip uomo nero atau gatto mammone. Di beberapa desa ia punya saudari musim dingin, sa Mama 'e su Frittu, serta sosok sejenis untuk api dan angin.",
      "en": "She was used to keep children indoors in the heat and also explained fever from sunstroke, much like the uomo nero or gatto mammone. Some villages give her a winter sister, sa Mama 'e su Frittu, and similar figures for fire and wind.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mama-e-su-sole-c02",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c05",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c06",
        "mama-e-su-sole-c07"
      ]
    }

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c01",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "Mama 'e su Sole è una creatura tipica del folklore sardo, nata per mettere in guardia i bambini dal rischio di restare al sole nelle ore più calde della giornata.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mama 'e su Sole adalah makhluk khas cerita rakyat Sardinia yang muncul untuk memperingatkan anak-anak akan bahaya berada di bawah matahari pada jam-jam terpanas.",
        "en": "Mama 'e su Sole is a creature typical of Sardinian folklore, born to warn children against staying in the sun in the hottest hours of the day."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c02",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "Concettualmente è simile all'uomo nero o al gatto mammone.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Secara konsep ia mirip uomo nero atau gatto mammone.",
        "en": "Conceptually it resembles the uomo nero or the gatto mammone."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c03",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "(la Madre del Sole) è identificata con una donna bellissima che appare d'estate, dopo mezzogiorno fino circa alle 16 del pomeriggio (le ore più calde della giornata).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sa Mama 'e su Sole (Ibu Matahari) diidentikkan dengan perempuan sangat cantik yang muncul pada musim panas, dari tengah hari sampai sekitar pukul 16, jam-jam terpanas.",
        "en": "Sa Mama 'e su Sole (the Mother of the Sun) is identified with a very beautiful woman who appears in summer from noon until about 16:00, the hottest hours."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c04",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "La leggenda dice che se la Mama 'e su Sole incontra un bambino per strada a giocare, disubbidendo ai genitori, lo rincorreva fino a raggiungerlo, gli tocca la fronte lasciandogli un segno e subito il bambino viene assalito da una febbre (halentura), che dura alcuni giorni.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, bila ia menemukan anak yang bermain di jalan karena tidak menuruti orang tuanya, ia mengejarnya, menyentuh dahinya hingga meninggalkan tanda, dan anak itu segera terserang demam (halentura) selama beberapa hari.",
        "en": "The legend says that if she meets a child playing in the street against its parents' wishes, she chases it down, touches its forehead leaving a mark, and the child is at once seized by a fever (halentura) lasting some days."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c05",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "Venne creato questo personaggio, anche per spiegare il fenomeno della febbre, dovuta in seguito a una prolungata esposizione al sole in un periodo di forte canicola.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh ini juga diciptakan untuk menjelaskan demam akibat terlalu lama terpapar matahari pada masa panas terik.",
        "en": "The figure was also created to explain fever caused by long exposure to the sun during a heatwave."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c06",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "si narra che esistesse anche sa Mama 'e su Frittu [pron. sa Mamma 'e su Vrittu] (la Madre del Freddo), sua sorella e controparte invernale.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di beberapa desa Sardinia diceritakan pula adanya sa Mama 'e su Frittu (Ibu Dingin), saudarinya dan padanannya pada musim dingin.",
        "en": "In some Sardinian villages there is said also to be sa Mama 'e su Frittu (the Mother of the Cold), her sister and winter counterpart."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c07",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s1",
      "quote": "Poi c'erano sa Mama 'e su Fogu [pron. sa Mamma 'e su Vógu] (la Madre del Fuoco), per spiegare ai bimbi il fenomeno degli incendi, e sa Mama 'e su Ventu [pron. sa Mamma 'e su Ventu] (la Madre del Vento), la quale appariva quando soffiavano il forte Maestrale o la Tramontana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada pula sa Mama 'e su Fogu (Ibu Api) untuk menjelaskan kebakaran kepada anak-anak, dan sa Mama 'e su Ventu (Ibu Angin) yang muncul saat angin Maestrale atau Tramontana bertiup kencang.",
        "en": "There were also sa Mama 'e su Fogu (the Mother of Fire), to explain fires to children, and sa Mama 'e su Ventu (the Mother of the Wind), who appeared when the strong Maestrale or Tramontana blew."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-e-su-sole-c08",
      "source_id": "mama-e-su-sole-s2",
      "quote": "Custa paristoria fit connotta in cada bidda peri tottu sa Sardigna.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Sardinia menyebut kisah ini dikenal di setiap desa di seluruh Sardinia.",
        "en": "The Sardinian-language article says this tale was known in every village throughout Sardinia."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mandrònius adalah raksasa setinggi sekitar tiga meter dari mitologi Gascon Val d'Aran yang memimpin penduduk lembah melawan bangsa Romawi.",
    "en": "Mandrònius is a giant about three metres tall from the Gascon mythology of the Val d'Aran who led the valley's people against the Romans.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mandronius-c01",
      "mandronius-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mandrònius adalah raksasa mitologi Gascon dari Val d'Aran, lelaki setinggi sekitar tiga meter yang tinggal di gua dekat Betlan. Ia memimpin penduduk lembah melawan bangsa Romawi dan menang dalam beberapa pertempuran. Ia menikahi perempuan Aran dan mempunyai seorang putri; ketika bangsa Romawi menculik perempuan lembah, ia menghancurkan perkemahan mereka dan mengirim telinga para prajurit ke Roma.",
      "en": "Mandrònius is a giant of the Gascon mythology of the Val d'Aran, a man about three metres tall who lived in a cave near Betlan. He led the valley's people against the Romans and won several battles. He married an Aranese woman and had a daughter; when the Romans carried off the valley's women, he wrecked their camp and sent the soldiers' ears to Rome.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mandronius-c01",
        "mandronius-c02",
        "mandronius-c03",
        "mandronius-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kisah kematiannya berbeda-beda: wafat tenang di guanya atau dibunuh penduduk Garòs. Konon kerangkanya ditemukan petani pada akhir abad ke-19 dan tengkoraknya sempat disimpan di menara gereja Garòs. Kini guanya menjadi bagian rute wisata budaya.",
      "en": "Accounts of his death differ: a peaceful end in his cave, or murder by the people of Garòs. A farmer reportedly found his skeleton in the late 19th century, and the skull was kept for a while in the Garòs church tower. Today his cave is part of cultural tours.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mandronius-c04",
        "mandronius-c05",
        "mandronius-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "title": "Gigant Mandrònius",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (oc)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "oc",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c01",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "Mandrònius, o Mandròni en versió aranesa –Mandroni en català–, és un gegant de la mitologia gascona pròpia de la Vall d'Aran.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mandrònius (Mandròni dalam versi Aran, Mandroni dalam bahasa Katalan) adalah raksasa dalam mitologi Gascon khas Val d'Aran.",
        "en": "Mandrònius (Mandròni in the Aranese version, Mandroni in Catalan) is a giant of the Gascon mythology of the Val d'Aran."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c02",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "Segons diu la llegenda, Mandrònius era un home d'aproximadament tres metres d'altura. Es diu que vivia en una cova a prop de Betlan i va liderar els habitants de la Vall d'Aran en la lluita contra els romans, obtenint victòries en diverses batalles i guanyant-se el respecte per la seva valentia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, Mandrònius adalah lelaki setinggi sekitar tiga meter yang tinggal di gua dekat Betlan dan memimpin penduduk Val d'Aran melawan bangsa Romawi, menang dalam beberapa pertempuran dan dihormati karena keberaniannya.",
        "en": "By the legend Mandrònius was a man about three metres tall who lived in a cave near Betlan and led the people of the Val d'Aran against the Romans, winning several battles and respect for his courage."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c03",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "Mandrònius es va enamorar d'una dona aranesa i van tenir una filla junts. Quan els romans van segrestar les dones de la vall, Mandrònius es va venjar devastant el campament enemic i enviant les orelles dels soldats com a advertència a Roma.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia jatuh cinta pada perempuan Aran dan mempunyai seorang putri; ketika bangsa Romawi menculik perempuan lembah itu, ia membalas dengan menghancurkan perkemahan musuh dan mengirim telinga para prajurit ke Roma sebagai peringatan.",
        "en": "He fell in love with an Aranese woman and they had a daughter; when the Romans abducted the valley's women, he took revenge by wrecking the enemy camp and sending the soldiers' ears to Rome as a warning."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c04",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "La seva mort és motiu d'especulació: algunes versions suggereixen que va morir pacíficament a la seva cova juntament amb la seva família, mentre que altres indiquen que va ser assassinat pels habitants de Garòs, que el consideraven una amenaça.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kematiannya diperdebatkan: sebagian versi menyebut ia wafat dengan tenang di guanya bersama keluarganya, versi lain menyebut ia dibunuh penduduk Garòs yang menganggapnya ancaman.",
        "en": "His death is debated: some versions say he died peacefully in his cave with his family, others that the people of Garòs, who saw him as a threat, killed him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c05",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "Es conta que el seu esquelet va ser descobert per un pagès a finals del segle xix, i la calavera es va conservar a la torre de l'església de Garòs durant algun temps. Se li atribuïen poders sobrenaturals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon kerangkanya ditemukan seorang petani pada akhir abad ke-19, dan tengkoraknya untuk sementara disimpan di menara gereja Garòs; kekuatan gaib dikaitkan dengannya.",
        "en": "His skeleton is said to have been found by a farmer in the late 19th century, and the skull was kept for a time in the tower of the church of Garòs; supernatural powers were attributed to it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c06",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s1",
      "quote": "La història del Gegant Mandrònius és recordada a la Vall d'Aran, i alguns guies inclouen avui la visita a la cova on es creu que va viure com a part dels itineraris culturals a la zona.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah Raksasa Mandrònius masih dikenang di Val d'Aran, dan sebagian pemandu kini memasukkan kunjungan ke gua yang diyakini tempat tinggalnya dalam rute wisata budaya.",
        "en": "The story of the Giant Mandrònius is remembered in the Val d'Aran, and some guides now include a visit to the cave he is believed to have lived in on cultural tours."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mandronius-c07",
      "source_id": "mandronius-s2",
      "quote": "Eth Gigant Mandrònius ei un personatge dera mitologia aranesa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Oksitan (Aran) menyebut Raksasa Mandrònius tokoh mitologi Aran.",
        "en": "The Occitan (Aranese) article calls the Giant Mandrònius a figure of Aranese mythology."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {

        "id": "Kematian Mandrònius",
        "en": "Mandrònius's death"
      },

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mommotti adalah sosok penakut anak dalam tradisi Sardinia, lelaki bermantel hitam bertudung yang berkeliling rumah mencari anak nakal untuk dibawa pergi.",
    "en": "The mommotti is a Sardinian bogeyman, a man in a black hooded cloak who goes from house to house looking for naughty children to carry off.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mommotti-c01",
      "mommotti-c02",
      "mommotti-c03",
      "mommotti-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mommotti, juga bobbotti atau momotti, adalah sosok khayalan Sardinia untuk menakut-nakuti anak, mirip uomo nero atau raksasa jahat yang membawa pergi anak nakal. Ia lelaki berjanggut lebat bermantel hitam panjang bertudung, bertongkat berbonggol dan bersepatu bot usang, yang menguping dari rumah ke rumah; di sebagian Sardinia ia dikaitkan dengan iblis.",
      "en": "The mommotti, also bobbotti or momotti, is an imaginary Sardinian figure for scaring children, like the uomo nero or a wicked ogre who takes away naughty ones. He is a thick-bearded man in a long black hooded cloak, with a knotty stick and worn boots, who eavesdrops from house to house; in parts of Sardinia he is tied to the devil.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mommotti-c01",
        "mommotti-c02",
        "mommotti-c03",
        "mommotti-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal namanya diperdebatkan: topeng Mamuthones yang menurut Raffaele Ballore berakar pra-Kristen, kisah perompak Arab bernama Mohammed di selatan, atau keluarga penculik Marotta dari Korsika di utara.",
      "en": "The origin of the name is disputed: the Mamuthones masks, which Raffaele Ballore thinks have pre-Christian roots, a tale of an Arab raider named Mohammed in the south, or the Marotta kidnapping family of Corsica in the north.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mommotti-c05",
        "mommotti-c06",
        "mommotti-c07",
        "mommotti-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],
